asambaddhakathālāpo' satpralāpa itīritaḥ ||3.179||
anyārthaṃ vacanaṃ hāsyakaraṃ vyāhāra ucyate |
doṣā  guṇā guṇā doṣā  yatra syurmṛdavaṃ hi tat ||3.180||
evamāmukhamāyojya sūtradhāre sahānuge |
niṣkrānte' thattadākṣiptaiḥ pātrairvastu prapañcayet ||3.181||
vastu sarvaṃ dvidhā sūcyamasūcyamiti bhedataḥ |
rasahīnaṃ bhavedatra vastu tatsūcyamucyate ||3.182||
yadvastu nīrasaṃ tattu sūcayetsūcakāstvamī |
viṣkambhacūlikāṅkāsyāṅkāvatārapraveśakāḥ ||3.183||
tatra viṣkambho bhūtabhāvivastvaṃśasūcakaḥ |
amukhyapātraracitaḥ saṅkṣepaikaprayojanaḥ ||3.184||
sa śuddho miśra ityukto miśraḥ syānnīcamadhyamaiḥ |
so' yaṃ ceṭīnaṭācāryasaṃlāpaparikalpitaḥ ||3.185||
mālavikāgnimitrasya prathamāṅke nirūpyatām |
śuddhaḥ kevalamadhyo' yamekānekakṛto dvidhā ||3.186||
ratnāvalyāmekaśuddhaḥ prāptayaugandharāyaṇaḥ |
anekaśuddho viṣkambhaḥ ṣaṣṭhāṅke' nargharāghave ||3.187||
nirūpyatāṃ samprayukto mālyavacchukasāraṇaiḥ |
vandimāgadhasūtādyaiḥ pratisīrāntarasthitaiḥ ||3.188||
arthopakṣepaṇaṃ yattu kriyate sā hi cūlikā |
sā dvidhā cūlikā  khaṇḍacūlikā  ceti bhedataḥ ||3.189||
pātrairyavanikāntaḥsthaiḥ kevalaṃ yā tu nirmitā |
ādāvaṅkasya madhye vā cūlikā nāma sā smṛtā ||3.190||
praveśanirgamābhāvādiyamaṅkādbahirgatā |
raṅganepathasaṃsthāyipātrasaṃlāpavistaraiḥ ||3.191||
ādau kevalamaṅkasya kalpitā khaṇḍacūlikā |
praveśanirgamāprāpteriyamaṅkādbahirgatā ||3.192||
enāṃ viṣkambhamevānye prāhurnaitanmataṃ mama |
apraviṣṭasya saṃlāpo viṣkambhe na hi yujyate ||3.193||
tadviṣkambhaśiraskatvānmateyaṃ khaṇḍacūlikā |
pūrvāṅkānte sampraviṣṭaiḥ pātrairbhāvyaṅkavastunaḥ ||3.194||
sūcanaṃ tadavicchityai yattadaṅkāsyamīritam |
yathā hi vīracarite dvitīyāṅkāvasānake ||3.195||
praviṣṭena sumantreṇa sūcitaṃ rāmavigrahe |
vasiṣṭhaviśvāmitrādisamābhāṣaṇalakṣaṇam ||3.196||
vastūttarāṅke pūrvārthāvicchedenaiva kalpitam |
aṅkāvatāraḥ pātrāṇāṃ pūrvakāryānuvartinām ||3.197||
avibhāgena sarveṣāṃ bhāvinyaṅke praveśanam |
dvitīyāṅke mālavikāgnimitre sa nirūpyatām ||3.198||
pātreṇāṅkapraviṣṭena kevalaṃ sūcitatvataḥ |
bhavedaṅkādabāhyatvamaṅkāsyāṅkāvatārayoḥ ||3.199||
yannīcaiḥ kevalaṃ pātrairbhāvibhūtārthasūcanam |
aṅkayorubhayormadhye sa vijñeyaḥ praveśakaḥ ||3.200||
so' yaṃ ceṭidvayālāpasaṃvidhānopakalpitaḥ |
mālatīmādhave prājñairdvitīyāṅke nirūpyatām ||3.201||
asūcyaṃ tu śubhodāttarasabhāvanirantaram |
prārambhe yadyasūcyaṃ syādaṅkamevātra kalpayet ||3.202||
rasālaṅkāravastūnāmupalālanakāṅkṣiṇām |
jananyaṅkavadādhārabhūtatvādaṅka ucyate ||3.203||
aṅkastu pañcaṣairdvitrairaṅgino' ṅgasya vastunaḥ |
rasasya vā samālambabhūtaiḥ pātrairmanoharaḥ ||3.204||
saṃvidhānaviśeṣaḥ syāttatrāsūcyaṃ prapañcayet |
asūcyaṃ taddvidhā dṛśyaṃ śrāvyaṃ cādyaṃ tu darśayet ||3.205||
dvedhā dvitīyaṃ svagataṃ prakāśaṃ ceti bhedataḥ |
svagataṃ svaikavijñeyaṃ prakāśaṃ taddvidhā bhavet ||3.206||
sarvaprakāśaṃ niyataprakāśaṃ ceti bhedataḥ |
sarvaprakāśaṃ sarveṣāṃ sthitānāṃ śravaṇocitam ||3.207||
dvitīyaṃ tu sthiteṣvapyeṣvekasya śravaṇocitam |
dvidhā vibhāvyate' nyacca janāntamapavāritam ||3.208||
tripatākākareṇānyānapavāryāntarā kathām |
anyenāmantraṇaṃ yatsyāttajjanāntikamucyate ||3.209||
rahasyaṃ kathyate' nyasya parāvṛtyāpavāritam |
itthaṃ śrāvyaṃ ca dṛśyaṃ ca prayujya susamāhitaiḥ ||3.210||
pātrairniṣkramaṇaṃ kāryamaṅkānte samameva hi |
aṅkacchedaśca kartavyaḥ kālāvasthānurodhataḥ ||3.211||
dinārdhadinayoryogyamaṅke vastu pravartayet |
aṅkaprasaṅgādgarbhāṅkalakṣaṇaṃ vakṣyate mayā ||3.212||
rasanāyakavastūnāṃ mahotkarṣāya kovidaiḥ |
aṅkasya madhye yo' ṅkaḥ syādasau garbhāṅka īritaḥ ||3.213||
vastusūcakanāndīko diṅmātramukhasaṅgataḥ |
arthopakṣepakairhīnaścūlikāparivarjitaiḥ ||3.214||
aneṣyadvastuviṣayaḥ pātraiśtricaturairyutaḥ |
nātiprapañcetivṛttaḥ svādhārāṅkāṅgaśobhitaḥ ||3.215||
prastutārthānubandhī ca pātraniṣkramaṇāvadhiḥ |
prathamāṅke na kartavyaḥ so' yaṃ kāvyaviśāradaiḥ ||3.216||
so' yamuttararāme tu rasotkarṣāya kathyatām |
neturutkarṣako jñeyo bālarāmāyaṇe tvayam ||3.217||
amogharāghave so' yaṃ vastūtkarṣaikakāraṇam |
nāṭake' ṅkā na kartavyāḥ pañcanyūnā daśādhikāḥ ||3.218||
tadīdṛśaguṇopetaṃ nāṭakaṃ bhuktimuktidam |

tathā ca bharataḥ
dharmārthasādhanaṃ nāṭyaṃ sarvaduḥkhāpanodakṛt |
āsevadhvaṃ tadṛṣayastasyotthānaṃ tu nāṭakam ||iti||

nāṭakasya tu pūrṇādibhedāḥ kecana kalpitāḥ ||3.219||
teṣāṃ nātīva ramyatvādaparīkṣākṣamatvataḥ |
muninānādṛtatvācca tānuddeṣṭumudāsmahe ||3.220||

atha prakaraṇam
yatretivṛttamutpādyaṃ dhīraśāntaśca nāyakaḥ |
rasaḥ pradhānaṃ śṛṅgāraḥ śeṣaṃ nāṭakavadbhavet ||3.221||
taddhi prakaraṇaṃ śuddhaṃ dhūrtaṃ miśraṃ ca tattridhā |
kulastrīnāyakaṃ śuddhaṃ mālatīmādhavādikam ||3.222||
gaṇikānāyikaṃ dhūrtaṃ kāmadattāhvayādikam |
kitavadhyputakārādivyāpāraṃ tvatra kalpayet ||3.223||
miśraṃ tatkulajāveśye kalpite yatra nāyike |
dhūrtaśuddhakramopetaṃ tanmṛcchakaṭikādikam ||3.224||
nāṭikā tvanayorbhedo na pṛthagrūpakaṃ bhavet |
prakhyātaṃ nṛpatervṛttaṃ nāṭakādāhṛtaṃ yataḥ ||3.225||
buddhikalpitavastutvaṃ tathā prakaraṇādapi |
vimarśasandhirāhityaṃ bhedakaṃ cenna tanmatam ||3.226||
ratnāvalyādike lakṣye tatsandherapi darśanāt |
strīprāyacaturaṅkādibhedakaṃ cenna tanmatam ||3.227||
ekadvitryaṅkapātrādibhedenānantatā yataḥ |
devīvaśātsaṅgamena bhedaścettanna yujyate |
mālavikāgnimitrādau nāṭikātvaprasaṅgataḥ ||3.228||
prakaraṇikānāṭikayoranusaraṇīyā hi nāṭikāsaraṇiḥ |
ata eva bharatamuninā nāṭyaṃ daśadhā nirūpitaṃ pūrvam ||3.229||
khyātena vā kalpitena vastunā prākṛtairnaraiḥ |
anvitaḥ kaiśikīhīnaḥ sāttvatyārabhaṭīmṛduḥ ||3.230||
strīṇāṃ vilāpavyāpārairupetaḥ karuṇāśrayaḥ |
nānāsaṅgrāmasaṃnāhaprahāramaraṇotkaṭaḥ ||3.231||
mukhanirvāhavān yaḥ syādekadvitryaṅka icchayā |
utsṛṣṭikāṅkaḥ sa jñeyaḥ saviṣkambhapraveśakaḥ ||3.232||
asminnamaṅgalaprāye kuryānmaṅgalamantataḥ |
prayojyasya vadhaḥ kāryaḥ punarujjīvanāvadhiḥ ||3.233||
ujjīvanādapyadhikaṃ manorathaphalo' pi vā |
vijñeyamasya lakṣyaṃ tu karuṇākandalādikam ||3.234||
khātetivṛttasampanno niḥsahāyakanāyakaḥ |
yukto daśāvaraihkhyātairuddhataiḥ pratināyakaiḥ ||3.235||
vimarśagarbharahito bhāratyārabhaṭīsphuṭaḥ |
hāsyaśṛṅgārarahita ekāṅko raudrasaṃśrayaḥ ||3.236||
ekavāsaravṛttāntaḥ prāptaviṣkambhacūlikaḥ |
astrīnimittasamaro vyāyogaḥ kathito budhaiḥ ||3.237||
vijñeyamasya lakṣyaṃ tu dhanañjayajayādikam |
svasya vānyasya vā vṛttaṃ viṭena nipuṇoktinā ||3.238||
śauryasaubhāgyasaṃstutyā vīraśṛṅgārasūcakam |
buddhikalpitamekāṅkaṃ mukhanirvahaṇānvitam ||3.239||
varṇyate bhāratīvṛttyā yatra taṃ bhāṇamīrate |
ekapātraprayojye' smin kuryādākāśabhāṣitam ||3.240||
anyenānuktamapyanyo vacaḥ śrutveva yadvadet |
iti kiṃ bhaṇasītyetadbhavedākāśabhāṣitam ||3.241||
lāsyāṅgāni daśaitasmin saṃyojyānyatra tāni tu |
geyapadaṃ sthitapāṭhyamāsīnaṃ puṣpagandhikā ||3.242||
pracchedakastrimūḍhaṃ ca saindhavākhyaṃ dvimūḍhakam |
uttamottamakaṃ cānyaduktapratyuktameva ca ||3.243||
vīṇādivādanenaiva sahitaṃ yatra bhāvyate |
lalitaṃ nāyikāgītaṃ tadgeyapadamucyate ||3.244||
cañcatpuṭādinā vākyābhinayo nāyikākṛtaḥ |
bhūmicārīpracāreṇa sthitapāṭhyaṃ taducyate ||3.245||
bhrūnetrapāṇicaraṇavilāsābhinayānvitam |
yojyamāsīnayā pāṭhyamāsīnaṃ tadudāhṛtam ||3.246||
nānāvidhena vādyena nānātālalayānvitam |
lāsyaṃ prayujyate yatra sā jñeyā puṣpagandhikā ||3.247||
anyāsaṅgamaśaṅkinyā nāyakasyātiroṣayā |
premacchedaprakaṭanaṃ lāsyaṃ pracchedakaṃ viduḥ ||3.248||
aniṣṭhuraślakṣṇapadaṃ  samavṛttairalaṅkṛtam |
nāṭyaṃ puruṣabhāvāḍhyaṃ trimūḍhakamudāhṛtam ||3.249||
deśabhāṣāviśeṣeṇa caladvalayaśṛṅkhalam |
lāsyaṃ prayujyate yatra tatsaindhavamiti smṛtam ||3.250||
cārībhirlalitābhiśca citrārthābhinayānvitam |
spaṣṭabhāvarasopetaṃ lāsyaṃ yattaddvimūḍhakam ||3.251||
aparijñātapārśvasthaṃ geyabhāvavibhūṣitam |
lāsyaṃ sotkaṇṭhavākyaṃ taduttamottamakaṃ bhavet ||3.252||
kopaprasādajanitaṃ sādhikṣepapadāśrayam |
vākyaṃ taduktapratyuktaṃ yūnoḥ praśnottarātmakam ||3.253||
śṛṅgāramañjarīmukhyamasyodāharaṇaṃ matam |
lāsyāṅgadaśakaṃ tatra lakṣyaṃ lakṣyavicakṣaṇaiḥ ||3.254||
prakhyātenetivṛttena nāyakairapi tadvidhaiḥ |
pṛthakprayojanāsaktairmilitairdevadānavaiḥ ||3.255||
yuktaṃ dvādaśabhirvīrapradhānaṃ kaiśikīmṛdu |
tryaṅkaṃ vimarśahīnaṃ ca kapaṭatrayasaṃyutam ||3.256||
trividravaṃ triśṛṅgāraṃ vidyātsamavakārakam |
mohātmako bhramaḥ proktaḥ kapaṭastrividhastvayam ||3.257||
sattvajaḥ śatrujo daivajanitaśceti sattvajaḥ |
krūraprāṇisamutpannaḥ śatrujastu raṇādijaḥ ||3.258||
vātyāvarṣādisambhūto daivajaḥ kapaṭaḥ smṛtaḥ |
udāharaṇameteṣāmāvege lakṣyatāṃ budhaiḥ ||3.259||
jīvagrāho' pi moho vā kapaṭādvidravastataḥ |
kapaṭatrayasambhūterayaṃ ca trividho mataḥ ||3.260||
dharmārthakāmasambaddhastridhā śṛṅgāra īritaḥ |
vratādijanitaḥ kāmo dharmaśṛṅgāra īritaḥ ||3.261||
pārvatīśivasambhogastadudāharaṇaṃ matam |
yatra kāmena sambaddhairarthairarthānubandhibhiḥ ||3.262||
bhujyamānaiḥ sukhaprāptirarthaśṛṅgāra īritaḥ |
sārvabhaumaphalaprāptihetunā vatsabhūpateḥ ||3.263||
ratnāvalyā samaṃ bhogo vijñeyā tadudāhṛtiḥ |
durādarasurāpānaparadārādikelijaḥ ||3.264||
tattadāsvādalalitaḥ kāmaśṛṅgāra īritaḥ |
tadudāharaṇaṃ prāyo dṛśyaṃ prasanādiṣu ||3.265||
śṛṅgāratritayaṃ tatra nātra bindupraveśakau |
mukhapratimukhe sandhī vastu dvādaśanāḍikam ||3.266||
prathame kalpayedaṅke nāḍikā ghaṭikādvayam |
mukhādisandhitrayavāṃścaturnāḍikavastukaḥ ||3.267||
dvitīyāṅkastṛtīyastu dvināḍikakathāśrayaḥ |
nirvimarśacatuḥsandhirevamaṅkāstrayaḥ smṛtāḥ ||3.268||
vīthīprahasanāṅgāni kuryādatra samāsataḥ |
prastāvanāyāḥ prastāve prokto vīthyaṅgavistaraḥ ||3.269||
daśa prahasanāṅgāni tatprasaṅge pracakṣmahe |
udāharaṇametasya payodhimathanādikam ||3.270||
sūcyapradhānaśṛṅgārā mukhanirvahaṇānvitā |
ekayojyā dviyojyā vā kaiśikīvṛttinirmitā ||3.271||
vīthyaṅgasahitaikāṅkā vīthīti kathitā budhaiḥ |
asyāṃ prāyeṇa lāsyāṅgadaśakaṃ yojayenna vā ||3.272||
sāmānyā parakīyā vā nāyikātrānurāgiṇī |
vīthyaṅgaprāyavṛttitvānnocitā kulapālikā ||3.273||
lakṣyamasyāstu vijñeyaṃ mādhavīvīthikādikam |
vastusandhyaṅkalāsyāṅgavṛttayo yatra bhāṇavat ||3.274||
raso hāsyaḥ pradhānaṃ syādetatprahasanaṃ matam |
viśeṣeṇa daśāṅgāni kalpayedatra tāni tu ||3.275||
avagalitāvaskandau vyavahāro vipralambha upapattiḥ |
bhayamanṛtaṃ vibhrāntirgadgadavākca pralāpaśca ||3.276||
pūrvamātmagṛhītasya samācārasya mohataḥ |
dūṣaṇaṃ tyajanaṃ cātra dvidhāvagalitaṃ matam ||3.277||
avaskandastvanekeṣāmayogyasyaikavastunaḥ |
sambandhābhāsakathanātsvasvayogyatvayojanā ||3.278||
vyavahārastu saṃvādo dvitrāṇāṃ hāsyakāraṇam |
vipralambho vañcanā syādbhūtāveśādikaitavāt ||3.279||
upapattistu sā proktā yatprasiddhasya vastunaḥ |
lokaprasiddhayā yuktyā sādhanaṃ hāsyahetunā ||3.280||
smṛtaṃ bhayaṃ tu nagaraśodhakādikṛto daraḥ |
anṛtaṃ tu bhavedvākyamasabhyastutigumphitam ||3.281||
tadevānṛtamityāhurapare svamatastuteḥ |
vastusāmyakṛto moho vibhrāntiriti gīyate ||3.282||
asatyaruditonmiśraṃ vākyaṃ gadgadavāgbhavet |
pralāpaḥ syādayogyasya yogyatvenānumodanam ||3.283||
śuddhaṃ kīrṇaṃ vaikṛtaṃ ca tacca prahasanaṃ tridhā |
śuddhaṃ śrotriyaśākhāderveṣabhāṣādisaṃyutam ||3.284||
ceṭaceṭījanavyāptaṃ tallakṣyaṃ tu nirūpyatām |
ānandakośapramukhaṃ tathā bhagavadajjukam ||3.285||
kīrṇaṃ tu sarvairvīthyaṅgaiḥ saṅkīrṇaṃ dhūrtasaṅkulam |
tasyodāharaṇaṃ jñeyaṃ bṛhatsaubhadrakādikam ||3.286||
yaccedaṃ kāmukādīnāṃ veṣabhāṣādisaṅgataiḥ |
ṣaṇḍatāpasavṛddhādyairyutaṃ tadvaikṛtaṃ bhavet ||3.287||
kalikeliprahasanapramukhaṃ tadudāhṛtam |
khyātetivṛttaṃ nirhāsyaśṛṅgāraṃ raudramudritam ||3.288||
sāttvatīvṛttiviralaṃ bhāratyārabhaṭīsphuṭam |
nāyakairuddhatairdevayakṣarākṣasapannagaiḥ ||3.289||
gandharvabhūtavetālasiddhavidyādharādibhiḥ |
samanvitaṃ ṣoḍaśabhirnyāyamārgaṇanāyakam ||3.290||
caturbhirāṅkairanvītaṃ nirvimarśakasandhibhiḥ |
nirghātolkoparāgādighorakrūrājisambhramam ||3.291||
sapraveśakaviṣkambhacūlikaṃ hi ḍimaṃ viduḥ |
asyodāharaṇaṃ jñeyaṃ vīrabhadravijṛmbhitam ||3.292||
yatretivṛttaṃ miśraṃ syātsaviṣkambhapraveśakam |
catvāro' ṅkā nirvimarśagarbhāḥ syuḥ sandhayastrayaḥ ||3.293||
dhīroddhattaśca prakhyāto divyo martyo' pi nāyakaḥ |
divyastriyamanicchantīṃ kanyāṃ vāhartumudyataḥ ||3.294||
strīnimittājisaṃrambhaḥ pañcaṣāḥ pratināyakāḥ |
rasā nirbhayabībhatsā vṛttayaḥ kaiśikīṃ vinā ||3.295||
svalpastasyāḥ praveśo vā so' yamīhāmṛgo mataḥ |
vyājānnivārayedatra saṅgrāmaṃ bhīṣaṇakramam ||3.296||
tasyodāharaṇaṃ jñeyaṃ prājñairmāyākuraṅgikā |
itthaṃ śrīsiṃhabhūpena sarvalakṣaṇaśālinā ||3.297||
sarvalakṣaṇasampūrṇo lakṣito rūpakakramaḥ |

atha rūpakanirmāṇaparijñānopayoginī ||3.298||
śrīsiṃhadharaṇīśena paribhāṣā nirūpyate |
paribhāṣātra maryādā pūrvācāryopakalpitā ||3.299||
sā hi nauratigambhīraṃ vivikṣornāṭyasāgaram |
eṣā ca bhāṣānirdeśanāmabhistrividhā matā ||3.300||
tatra bhāṣā dvidhā bhāṣā vibhāṣā ceti bhedataḥ |
caturdaśa vibhāṣāḥ syuḥ prācyādyā vākyavṛttayaḥ ||3.301||
āsāṃ saṃskārarāhityādviniyogo na kathyate |
uttamādiṣu taddeśavyavahārātpratīyatām ||3.302||
bhāṣā dvidhā saṃskṛtā ca prākṛtī ceti bhedataḥ |
kaumārapāṇinīyādisaṃskṛtā saṃskṛtā matā ||3.303||
iyaṃ tu devatādīnāṃ munīnāṃ nāyakasya ca |
liṅgināṃ ca viṭādīnmanīcānāṃ prayujyate ||3.304||
prakṛteḥ saṃskṛtāyāstu vikṛtiḥ prākṛtī matā ||3.305||
ṣaḍvidhā sā prākṛtaṃ ca śaurasenī ca māgadhī |
paiśācī cūlikā paiśācyapabhraṃśa iti kramāt ||3.306||
atra tu prākṛtaṃ strīṇāṃ sarvāsāṃ niyataṃ bhavet |
kvacicca devī gaṇikā mantrijā ceti yoṣitām ||3.307||
yoginyapsarasoḥ śilpakāriṇyā api saṃskṛtam |
ye nīcāḥ karmaṇā jātyā teṣāṃ prākṛtamucyate ||3.308||
chadmaliṅgavatāṃ tadvajjainānāmiti kecana |
adhame madhyame cāpi śaurasenī prayujyate ||3.309||
dhīvarādyatinīceṣu māgadhī ca niyujyate |
rakṣaḥpiśācanīceṣu paiśācīdvitayaṃ bhavet ||3.310||
apabhraṃśastu caṇḍālayavanādiṣu yujyate |
nāṭakādāvapabhraṃśavinyāsasyāsahiṣṇavaḥ ||3.311||
anye caṇḍālakādīnāṃ māgadhyādīn prayuñjate |
sarveṣāṃ kāraṇavaśātkāryo bhāṣāvyatikramaḥ ||3.312||
māhātmyasya paribhraṃśaṃ madasyātiśayaṃ tathā |
pracchādanaṃ ca vibhrāntiṃ yathālikhitavācanam ||3.313||
kadācidanuvādaṃ ca kāraṇāni pracakṣate |
sākṣādanāmagrāhyāṇāṃ janānāṃ pratisaṃjñayā ||3.314||
āhvānabhaṅgī nāṭyajñairnirdeśa iti gīyate |
sa tridhā pūjyasadṛśakaniṣṭhaviṣayatvataḥ ||3.315||
pūjyāstu devo munayo liṅginastatsamāstriyaḥ |
bahuśrutāśca bhagavacchabdavācyā bhavanti hi ||3.316||
āryeti brāhmaṇo vācyo vṛddhastāteti bhāṣyate |
upādhyāyeti cācāryo gaṇikā tvajjukākhyayā ||3.317||
mahārājeti bhūpālo vidvān bhāva itīryate |
chandato nāmabhirvācyā brāhmaṇaistu narādhipāḥ ||3.318||
deveti nṛpatirvācyo bhṛtyaiḥ prakṛtibhistathā |
sārvabhaumaḥ parijanairbhaṭṭabhaṭṭāraketi ca ||3.319||
vācyo rājeti munibhirapatyapratyayena vā |
vidūṣakeṇa tu prāyaḥ sakhe rājannitīcchayā ||3.320||
brāhmaṇaiḥ sacivo vācyo hyamātya saciveti ca |
śaiṣāirāryetyathāyuṣmaniti sārathinā rathī ||3.321||
tapasvisādhuśabdābhyāṃ praśāntaḥ paribhāṣyate |
svāmīti yuvarājastu kumāro bhartṛdārakaḥ ||3.322||
āvutteti svasurbhartā syāleti pṛtanāpatiḥ |
bhaṭṭinī svāminī devī tathā bhaṭṭāriketi ca ||3.323||
paricārajanairvācyā yoṣito rājavallabhāḥ |
rājñā tu mahiṣī vācyā devītyanyāḥ priyā iti ||3.324||
sarveṇa patnī tvāryeti piturnāmnā sutasya vā |
tātapādā iti pitā mātāmbeti sutena tu ||3.325||
jyeṣṭhāstvāryā iti bhrātrā tathā syurmātulādayaḥ |
sadṛśaḥ sadṛśo vācyo vayasyetyāhvayena vā ||3.326||
haleti sakhyā tu sakhī kathanīyā sakhīti vā |
sutaśiṣyakanīyāṃso vācyā gurujanena hi ||3.327||
vatsaputrakadīrghāyustātajāteti saṃjñayā |
anyaḥ kanīyānāryeṇa janena paribhāṣyate ||3.328||
śilpādhikāranāmabhyāṃ bhadra bhadramukheti |
vācye nīcātinīce tu haṇḍe hañje iti kramāt ||3.329||
bhartrā vācyāḥ svasvanāmnā bhṛtyāḥ śilpocitena vā |
evamādi prakāreṇa yojyā nirdeśayojanā ||3.330||
lokaśāstrāvirodhena vijñeyā kāvyakovidaiḥ |
anuktanāmnaḥ prakhyāte kañcukiprabhṛterapi ||3.331||
itivṛtte kalpite tu nāyakāderapi sphuṭam |
rasavastūpayogīni kavirnāmāni kalpayet ||3.332||
vinayandharabābhravyajayandharajayādikam |
kāryaṃ kañcukināṃ nāma prāyo viśvāsasūcakam ||3.333||
latālaṅkārapuṣpādivastūnāṃ lalitātmanām |
nāmabhirguṇasiddhairceṭīnāṃ nāma kalpayet ||3.334||
karabhaḥ kalahaṃsaścetyādi nāmānujīvinām |
karpūracaṇḍakāmpilyetyādikaṃ nāma vandinām ||3.335||
subuddhivasubhūtyādimantriṇāṃ nāma kalpayet |
devarātaḥ somarāta iti nāma purodhasaḥ ||3.336||
śrīvatso gautamaḥ kautso gārgyo maudgalya ityapi |
vasantakaḥ kāpileya ityākhyeyo vidūṣakaḥ ||3.337||
pratāpavīravijayamānavikramasāhasaiḥ |
vasantabhūṣaṇottaṃsaśekharāṅkapadottaraiḥ ||3.338||
dhīrottarāṇāṃ netṝṇāṃ nāma kurvīta kovidaḥ |
candrāpīḍaḥ kāmapāla ityādyaṃ lalitātmanām ||3.339||
ugravarmā caṇḍasena ityādyuddhatacetasām |
dattasenāntanāmāni vaiśyānāṃ kalpayetsudhīḥ ||3.340||
karpūramañjarī candralekhā rāgataraṅgikā |
padmāvatīti prāyeṇa nāmnā vācyā hi nāyikā ||3.341||
devyastu dhāriṇīlakṣmīvasumatyādināmabhiḥ |
bhogavatī kāntimatī kamalā kāmavallarī ||3.342||
irāvatī haṃsapadītyādināmnā tu bhoginī |
viprakṣatraviśaḥ śarmavarmadattāntanāmabhiḥ ||3.343||
śikhaṇḍāṅgadacūḍāntanāmnā vidyādharādhipāḥ |
kuṇḍalānandaghaṇṭāntanāmnā kāpālikā janāḥ ||3.344||
yogasundarikā vaṃśaprabhā vikaṭamudrikā |
śaṅkhakeyūriketyādināmnā kāpālikastriyaḥ ||3.345||
ānandinī siddhimatī śrīmatī sarvamaṅgalā |
yaśovatī putravatītyādināmnā suvāsinī ||3.346||
ityādi sarvamālocya lakṣaṇaṃ kṛtabuddhinā |
kavinā kalpitaṃ kāvyamācandrārkaṃ prakāśate ||3.347||
lakṣyalakṣaṇanirmāṇavijñānakṛtabuddhibhiḥ |
parīkṣyatāmayaṃ grantho vimatsaramanīṣayā ||3.348||
bharatāgamapārīṇaḥ śrīmān siṃhamahīpatiḥ |
rasikaḥ kṛtavānevaṃ rasārṇavasudhākaram ||3.349||

saṃrambhādanapotasiṃhanṛpaterdhāṭīsamāṭīkane
niḥsāṇeṣu dhaṇaṃ dhaṇaṃ dhaṇamiti dhvānānusandhāyiṣu |
modante hi raṇaṃ raṇaṃ raṇamiti prauḍhāstadīyā bhaṭā
bhrāntiṃ yānti tṛṇaṃ tṛṇaṃ tṛṇamiti pratyarthipṛthvībhujaḥ ||3.350||

matvā dhātrā tulāyāṃ laghuriti dharaṇīṃ siṃhabhūpālacandre
sṛṣṭe tatrātigurvyāṃ tadupanidhitayā sthāpyamānaiḥ krameṇa |
cintāratnaughakalpadrumatatisurabhīmaṇḍalaiḥ pūritāntāpy
ūrdhvaṃ nītā laghimnā tadarikulaśataiḥ pūryate' dyāpi sā dyauḥ ||3.351||


iti śrīmadāndhramaṇḍalādhīśvarapratigaṇḍabhairavaśrīmadanapotanarendranandanabhujabalabhīmaśrīsiṃhabhūpālaviracite rasārṇavasudhākaranāmni nāṭyālaṅkāraśāstre bhāvakollāso nāma
tṛtīyo vilāsaḥ
||3||


samāptaścāyaṃ rasārṇavasudhākaraḥ

śrītoyaśailavasatiḥ sa tamālanīlo
jīyāddharirmunicakorasuśāradenduḥ |
lakṣmīstanastavakakuṅkumakardamaśrī
saṃliptanirmalaviśālabhujāntarālaḥ ||

malayagirinivāsī māruto yacchatāṅgas
taruṇaśiśiraraśmiryatsuhṛtpuṇyakīrtiḥ |
carati ciramanaṅgaḥ kvāpi kari apyadṛśyaḥ
sa jayatu rasikaughairvanditaḥ pañcabāṇaḥ ||

aśeṣāṇāṃ dvijanuṣāmāśīrvādaparamparā |
taraṅgayatu kalyāṇaṃ kavīnāṃ cāyurāyatam ||







rasārṇavasudhākaraḥ

the rasārṇavasudhākara of siṃhabhūpāla. criticallyeditedwithintroductionandnotesbyt. venkatacharya. madrasadyarlibraryandresearchcentre, 1979.




śrīsiṃhabhūpālaviracito
rasārṇavasudhākaraḥ

(1)
prathamo vilāsaḥ

rañjakollāsaḥ

śṛṅgāravīrasauhārdaṃ maugdhyavaiyātyasaurabham |
lāsyatāṇḍavasaujanyaṃ dāmpatyaṃ tadbhajāmahe ||1.1||
vīṇāṅkitakarāṃ vande vānīmeṇīdṛśaṃ sadā |
sadānandamayīṃ devīṃ sarojāsanavallabhām ||1.2||
asti kiñcitparaṃ vastu paramānandakandalam |
kamalākucakāṭhinyakutūhalibhujāntaram ||1.3||
tasya pādāmbujājjāto varṇo vigatakalmaṣaḥ |
yasya sodaratāṃ prāptaṃ bhagīrathatapaḥphalam ||1.4||
tatra recarlavaṃśābdhiśaradrākāsudhākaraḥ |
kalānidhirudāraśrīrāsīddācayanāyakaḥ ||1.5||
yasyāsidhārāmārgeṇa durgeṇāpi raṇāṅgaṇe |
pāṇḍyarājagajānīkājjayalakṣmīrupāgatā ||1.6||
aṅganārāyaṇe yasmin bhavati śrīratisthirā |
bhūrabhūtkariṇī vaśyā duṣṭarājagajāṅkuśe ||1.7||
tasya bhāryā mahābhāgyā viṣṇoḥ śrīriva viśrutā |
pocamāmbā guṇodārā jātā tāmarasānvayāt ||1.8||

tayorabhūvan kṣitikalpavṛkṣāḥ
putrāstrayastrāsitavairivīrāḥ |
siṃhaprabhurvennamanāyakaśca
vīrāgraṇī recamahīpatiśca ||1.9||

kalāvekapado dharmo yairebhiścaraṇairiva |
sampūrṇapadatāṃ prāpya nākāṅkṣati kṛtaṃ yugam ||1.10||
tatra siṃhamahīpāle pālayatyakhilāṃ mahīm |
namatāmunnatiścitraṃ rājñāmanamatāṃ natiḥ ||1.11||

kṛṣṇaileśvarasaṃnidhau kṛtamahāsambhārameleśvare
vītāpāyamanekaśo vidadhatā brahmapratiṣṭhāpanam |
ānṛṇya samapādi yena vibhunā tattadguṇairātmano
nirmāṇātiśayaprayāsagarimavyāsaṅgini brahmaṇi ||1.12||

kṛtāntajihvākuṭilāṃ kṛpāṇīṃ
dṛṣṭvā yadīyāṃ trasatāmarīṇām |
svedodayaścetasi saṃcitānāṃ
mānoṣmaṇāmātanute praśāntim ||1.13||

śrīmān recamahīpatiḥ sucarito yasyānujanmā sphuṭaṃ
prāpto vīraguruprathāṃ pṛthutarāṃ vīrasya mudrākarīm |
labdhvā labdhakaṭhārirāyavirudaṃ rāhuttarāyāṅkitaṃ
putraṃ nāgayanāyakaṃ vasumatīvīraikacūḍāmaṇim ||1.14||

so' yaṃ siṃhamahīpālo vasudeva iti sphuṭam |
anantamādhavau yasya tanūjau lokarakṣakau ||1.15||

tatrānujo mādhavanāyakendro
digantarālaprathitapratāpaḥ |
yasyābhavan vaṃśakarā narendrās
tanūbhavā vedagirīndramukhyāḥ ||1.16||
tasyāgrajanmā bhuvi rājadoṣair
aprotabhāvādanapotasaṃjñām |
khyātāṃ dadhāti sma yathārthabhūtām
anantasajñāṃ ca mahīdharatvam ||1.17||

sodaryo balabhadramūrtiraniśaṃ devī priyā rukmiṇī
pradyumnastanayo' ppautranivaho yasyāniruddhādayaḥ |
so' yaṃ śrīpatirannapotanṛpatiḥ kiṃ cānanāmbhoruhe
dhatte cārusudarśanaśriyamasau satvātmahastāmbuje ||1.18||

bahusomasutaṃ kṛtvā bhūlokaṃ yatra rakṣati |
ekasomasutaṃ rakṣan svarlokaṃ lajjate hariḥ ||1.19||

somakulaparaśurāṃe
bhujabalabhīme' rigāyagobāle |
yatra ca jāgrati śāsati
jagatāṃ jāgarti nityakalyāṇam ||1.20||

hemādridānairdharaṇīsurāṇāṃ
hemācalaṃ hastagataṃ vidhāya |
yaścārusopānapathena cakre
śrīparvataṃ sarvajanāṅghrigamyam ||1.21||

yo naikavīroddalano' pyasaṅkhya
saṅkhyo' pyabhagnātmagatikramo' pi |
ajātisāṅkaryabhavo' pi citraṃ
dadhāti somānvayabhārgavāṅkam ||1.22||

dhāvaṃ dhāvaṃ ripunṛpatayo yuddharaṅgāpaviddhāḥ
khaḍge khaḍge phalitavapuṣaṃ yaṃ purastādvilokya |
pratyāvṛttā api tata ito vīkṣamāṇā yadīyaṃ
saṃmanyante sphuṭamavitathaṃ khaḍganārāyaṇāṅkam ||1.23||

annamāmbeti vikhyātā tasyāsīddharaṇīpateḥ |
devī śivā śivasyeva rājamaulermahojjvalā ||1.24||
śatrughnaṃ śrutakīrtiryā subhadrā yaśasārjunam |
ānandayati bhartāraṃ śyāmā rājānamujjvalam ||1.25||
tayorabhūtāṃ putrau dvāvādyo vedagirīśvaraḥ |
dvitīyastvadvitīyo' sau yaśasā siṃhabhūpatiḥ ||1.26||
atha śrīsiṃhabhūpālo dīrghāyurvasudhāmimām |
nijāṃsapīṭhe nirvyājaṃ kurute supratiṣṭhitām ||1.27||

ahīnajyābandhaḥ kanakaruciraṃ kārmukavaraṃ
balidhvaṃsī bāṇaḥ parapuramanekaṃ ca viṣayaḥ |
iti prāyo lokottarasamarasaṃnāhavidhinā
maheśo' yaṃ siṃhakṣitipa iti yaṃ jalpati janaḥ ||1.28||

yatra ca raṇasaṃnahini
tṛṇacaraṇaṃ nijapurācca niḥsaraṇam |
vanacaraṇaṃ taccaraṇaka
paricaraṇaṃ vā virodhināṃ śaraṇam ||1.29||

satāṃ prītiṃ kurvan kuvalayavikāsaṃ viracayan
kalāḥ kāntāḥ puṣṇan dadhadapi ca jaivātṛkakathām |
nitāntaṃ yo rājā prakaṭayati mitrodayamaho
tathā cakrānandānapi ca kamalollāsasuṣamām ||1.30||

tallabdhāni ghanāghanairatitarāṃ vārāṃ pṛṣantyambudhau
svātyāmeva hi śuktikāsu dadhate muktāni muktātmatām |
yaddānodakavipruṣastu sudhiyāṃ haste patantyo' bhavan
māṇikyāni mahāmbarāṇi bahuśo dhāmāni hemāni ca ||1.31||

nayanamayaṃ guṇamaguṇaṃ
padamapadaṃ nijamavetya ripubhūpāḥ |
yasya ca nayaguṇaviduṣo
vinamanti padāravindapīṭhāntam ||1.32||

prāṇānāṃ parirakṣaṇāya bahuśo vṛttiṃ madīyāṃ gatās
tvatsāmantamahībhujaḥ karuṇayā te rakṣaṇīyā iti |
karṇe varṇayituṃ nitāntasuhṛdo karṇāntaviśrāntayor
manye yasya dṛgantayoḥ parisaraṃ sā kāmadhenuḥ śritā ||1.33||

yuṣmābhiḥ pratigaṇḍabhairavaraṇe prāṇāḥ kathaṃ rakṣitā
ityantaḥpurapṛcchayā yadariṣu prāpteṣu lajjāvaśam |
śaṃsantyuttaramānanavyatikaravyāpārapāraṅgatā
gaṇḍāndolitakarṇakuṇḍalaharinmāṇikyavarṇāṅkurāḥ ||1.34||

mandārapārijātaka
candanasantānakalpamaṇisadṛśaiḥ |
anapotadācavallabha
vedagirisvāmimādadāmayasaṃjñaiḥ ||1.35||

ātmabhavairativibhavair
anitarajanasulabhadānamuditairbhuvi yaḥ |
ratnākara iva rājati
rājakarāracitasukamalollāsaḥ ||1.36||

yasyāḍhyaḥ prathamaḥ kumāratilakaḥ śrīannapoto guṇair
ekasyāgrajamātmarūpavibhave cāpe dvayoragrajam |
ārūḍhe tritayāgrajaṃ vijayate durvāradorvikrame
satyoktau caturagrajaṃ vitaraṇe kiṃ cāpi pañcāgrajam ||1.37||

yuddhe yasya kumāradācayavibhoḥ khaḍgāgradhārājale
majjanti pratipakṣabhūmipatayaḥ śauryoṣmasantāpitāḥ |
citraṃ tatpramadāḥ pranaṣṭatilakā vyākīrṇanīlālakāḥ
prabhraśyatkucakuṅkumāḥ parigalannetrāntakālāñjanāḥ ||1.38||

paripoṣiṇi yasya putraratne
dayite vallabharāyapūrṇacandre |
samudeti satāṃ prabhāvaśeṣaḥ
kamalānāmabhivardhanaṃ tu citram ||1.39||

etairanyaiśca tanayaiḥ so' yaṃ siṃhamahīpatiḥ |
ṣaḍbhiḥ pratiṣṭhāmayate svāmīvāṅgaiḥ susaṅgataiḥ ||1.40||

rājā sa rājācalanāmadheyām
adhyāsta vaṃśakramarājadhānīm |
satāṃ ca rakṣāmasatāṃ ca śikṣāṃ
nyāyānurodhādanusandadhānajñāḥ ||1.41||

vindhyaśrīśailamadhyakṣmāmaṇḍalaṃ pālayan sutaiḥ |
vaṃśapravartakairarthān bhuṅkte bhogapurandaraḥ ||1.42||

tasmin śāsati siṃhabhūmiramaṇe kṣmāmannapotātmaje
kāṭhinyaṃ kucamaṇḍale taralatā netrāñcale subhruvām |
vaiṣamyaṃ trivalīṣu mandapadatā līlālasāyāṃ gatau
kauṭilyaṃ cikureṣu kiṃ ca kṛśatā madhye paraṃ badhyate ||1.43||

so' haṃ kalyāṇarūpasya varṇotkarṣaikakāraṇam |
vidvatprasādanāhetorvakṣye nāṭyasya lakṣaṇam ||1.44||
purā purandarādyāste praṇamya caturānanam |
kṛtāñjalipuṭā bhūtvā papracchuḥ sarvavedinam ||1.45||
bhagavan śrotumicchāmaḥ śrāvyaṃ dṛśyaṃ manoharam |
dharmyaṃ yaśasyamarthyaṃ ca sarvaśilpapradarśanam ||1.46||
paraṃ pañcamamāmnāyaṃ sarvavarṇādhikārikam |
iti pṛṣṭaḥ sa tairbrahmā sarvavedānanusmaran ||1.47||
tebhyaśca sāramādāya nāṭyavedamathāsṛjat |
adhyāpya bharatācāryaṃ prajāpatirabhāṣata ||1.48||
saha putrairimaṃ vedaṃ prayogeṇa prakāśaya |
iti tena niyuktastu bharataḥ saha sūnubhiḥ ||1.49||
prāyojayatsudharmāyāmindrasyāgre' psarogaṇaiḥ |
sarvalokopakārāya nāṭyaśāstraṃ ca nirmame ||1.50||
tathā tadanusāreṇa śāṇḍilyaḥ kohalo' pi ca |
dattilaśca mataṅgaśca ye cānye tattanūdbhavāḥ ||1.51||
granthānnānāvidhāṃścakruḥ prakhyātāste mahītale |
teṣāmatigabhīratvādviprakīrṇakramatvataḥ ||1.52||
sampradāyasya vicchedāttadvidāṃ viralatvataḥ |
prāyo viralasañcārā nāṭyapaddhatirasphuṭā ||1.53||
tasmādasmatprayatno' yaṃ tatprakāśanalakṣaṇaḥ |
sāraikagrāhiṇāṃ cittamānandayati dhīmatām ||1.54||

nedānīntanadīpikā kimu tamaḥsaṅhātamunmūlayej
jyotsnā kiṃ na cakorapāraṇakṛte tatkālasaṃśobhinī |
bālaḥ kiṃ kamalākarān dinamaṇirnollāsayedañjasā
tatsampratyapi mādṛśāmapi vacaḥ syādeva samprītaye ||1.55||

svacchasvādurasādhāro vastucchāyāmanoharaḥ |
sevyaḥ suvarṇanidhivannāṭyamārgaḥ sanāyakaḥ ||1.56||
sāttvikādyairabhinayaiḥ prekṣakāṇāṃ yato bhavet |
naṭe nāyakatādātmyabuddhistannāṭyamucyate ||1.57||
rasotkarṣo hi nāṭyasya prāṇāstatsa nirūpyate |
vibhāvairanubhāvaiśca sāttvikairvyabhicāribhiḥ ||1.58||
ānīyamānaḥ svādutvaṃ sthāyī bhāvo rasaḥ smṛtaḥ |
tatra jñeyo vibhāvastu rasajñāpanakāraṇam ||1.59||
budhairjñeyo' yamālamba uddīpana iti dvidhā |
ādhāraviṣayatvābhyāṃ nāyako nāyikāpi ca ||1.60||
ālambanaṃ mataṃ tatra nāyako guṇavān pumān |
tadguṇāstu mahābhāgyamaudāryaṃ sthairyadakṣate ||1.61||
aujjvalyaṃ dhārmikatvaṃ ca kulīnatvaṃ ca vāgmitā |
kṛtajñatvaṃ nayajñatvaṃ śucitā mānaśālitā ||1.62||
tejasvitā kalāvattvaṃ prajārañjakatādayaḥ |
ete sādhāraṇāḥ proktāḥ nāyakasya guṇā budhaiḥ ||1.63||
sarvātiśāyirājyatvaṃ mahābhāgyamudāhṛtam |
yadviśrāṇanaśīlatvaṃ tadaudāryaṃ budhā viduḥ ||1.64||
vyāpāraṃ phalaparyantaṃ sthairymāhurmanīṣiṇaḥ |
duṣkare kṣiprakāritvaṃ dakṣatāṃ paricakṣate ||1.65||
aujjvalyaṃ nayanānandakāritvaṃ kathyate budhaiḥ |
dharmapravaṇacittatvaṃ dhārmikatvamitīryate ||1.66||
kule mahati sambhūtiḥ kulīnatvamudāhṛtam |
vāgmitā tu budhairuktā samayocitabhāṣitā ||1.67||
kṛtānāmupakārāṇāmabhjñatvaṃ kṛtajñatā |
sāmādyupāyacāturyaṃ nayajñatvamudāhṛtam ||1.68||
antaḥkaraṇaśuddhiryā śucitā sā prakīrtitā |
akārpaṇyasahiṣṇutvaṃ kathitā mānaśālitā ||1.69||
tejasvitvamavajñāderasahiṣṇutvamucyate |
kalāvattvaṃ nigaditaṃ sarvavidyāsu kauśalam ||1.70||
rañjakatvaṃ tu sakalacittāhlādanakāritā |
uktairguṇaiśca sakalairyuktaḥ syāduttamo netā ||1.71||
madhyaḥ katipayahīno bahuguṇahīno' dhamo nāma |
netā caturvidho' sau dhīrodāttaśca dhīralalitaśca ||1.72||
dhīrapraśāntanāmā tataśca dhīroddhataḥ khyātaḥ |
dayāvānatigambhīro vinītaḥ sattvasāravān ||1.73||
dṛḍhavratastitikṣāvānātmaślāghāparāṅmukhaḥ |
nigūḍhāhaṅkṛtirdhīrairdhīrodātta udāhṛtaḥ ||1.74||
dayātiśayaśālitvaṃ dayāvattvamudāhṛtam |
gāmbhīryamavikāraḥ syātsatyapi kṣobhakāraṇe ||1.75||
niścinto dhīralalitastaruṇo vanitāvaśaḥ |
śamaprakṛtikaḥ kleśasahiṣṇuśca vivecakaḥ ||1.76||
lalitādiguṇopeto vipro vā sacivo vaṇik |
dhīraśāntaścārudattamādhavādirudāhṛtaḥ ||1.77||
mātsaryavānahaṅkārī māyāvī roṣaṇaścalaḥ |
vikatthano bhārgavādirdhīroddhata udāhṛtaḥ ||1.78||
ete ca nāyakāḥ sarvarasasādhāraṇāḥ smṛtāḥ |
śṛṅgārāpekṣayā teṣāṃ traividhyaṃ kathyate budhaiḥ ||1.79||
patiścopapatiścaiva vaiśikaśceti bhedataḥ |
patistu vidhinā pāṇigrāhakaḥ kathyate budhaiḥ ||1.80||
caturdhā so' pi kathito vṛttyā kāvyavicakṣaṇaiḥ |
anukūlaḥ śaṭho dhṛṣṭo dakṣiṇaśceti bhedataḥ ||1.81||
anukūlastvekajāniḥ śaṭho gūḍhāparādhakṛt |
dhṛṣṭo vyaktānyayuvatībhogalakṣmāpi nirbhayaḥ ||1.82||
nāyikāsvapyanekāsu tulyo dakṣiṇa ucyate |
laṅghitācārayā yastu vināpi vidhinā striyā ||1.83||
saṅketaṃ nīyate prokto budhairupapatistu saḥ |
dākṣiṇyamānukūlyaṃ ca dhārṣṭyaṃ cāniyatatvataḥ ||1.84||
nocitānyasya śāṭhyaṃ syādanyacittatvasambhavāt |
rūpavān śīlasampannaḥ śāstrajñaḥ priyadarśanaḥ ||1.85||
kulīno matimān śūro ramyaveṣayuto yuvā |
adīnaḥ surabhistyāgī sahanaḥ priyabhāṣaṇaḥ ||1.86||
śaṅkavihīno mānī ca deśakālavibhāgavit |
dākṣyacāturyamādhuryasaubhāgyādibhiranvitaḥ ||1.87||
veśyopabhogarasiko yo bhavetsa tu vaiśikaḥ |
kalakaṇṭhādiko lakṣyo bhāṇādāveva vaiśikaḥ ||1.88||
sa tridhā kathyate jyeṣṭhamadhyanīcavibhedataḥ |
atha śṛṅgāranetṝṇāṃ sāhāyyakaraṇocitāḥ ||1.89||
nirūpyante pīṭhamardaviṭaceṭavidūṣakāḥ |
nāyakānucaro bhaktaḥ kiñcidūnaśca tadguṇaiḥ ||1.90||
pīṭhamarda iti khyātaḥ kupitastrīprasādakaḥ |
kāmatantrakalāvedī viṭa ityabhidhīyate ||1.91||
sandhānakuśalaśceṭaḥ kalahaṃsādiko mataḥ |
vikṛtāṅgavacoveṣairhāsyakārī vidūṣakaḥ ||1.92||
deśakālajñatā bhāṣāmādhuryaṃ ca vidagdhatā |
protsāhane kuśalatā yathoktakathanaṃ tathā ||1.93||
nigūḍhamantratetyādyāḥ sahāyānāṃ guṇā matāḥ |
netṛsādhāraṇaguṇairupetā nāyikā matā ||1.94||
svakīyā parakīyā ca sāmānyā ceti sā tridhā |
sampatkāle vipatkāle yā na muñcati vallabham ||1.95||
śīlārjavaguṇopetā sā svakīyā kathitā budhaiḥ |
sā ca svīyā tridhā mugdhā madhyā prauḍheti kathyate ||1.96||
mugdhā navavayaḥkāmā ratau vāmā mṛduḥ krudhi |
yatate rataceṣṭāyāṃ gūḍhaṃ lajjāmanoharam ||1.97||
kṛtāparādhe dayite vīkṣate rudatī satī |
apriyaṃ vā priyaṃ vāpi na kiñcidapi bhāṣate ||1.98||
samānalajjāmadanā prodyattāruṇyaśālinī |
madhyā kāmayate kāntaṃ mohāntasuratakṣamā ||1.99||
madhyā tridhā mānavṛtterdhīrādhīrobhayātmikā |
dhīrā tu vakti vakroktyā sotprāsaṃ sāgasaṃ priyam ||1.100||
adhīrā paruṣairvākyaiḥ khedayedvallabhaṃ ruṣā |
dhīrādhīra tu vakroktyā sabāṣpaṃ vadati priyam ||1.101||
sampūrṇayauvanonmattā pragalbhā rūḍhamanmathā |
dayitāṅge vilīneva yatate ratikeliṣu ||1.102||
rataprārambhamātre' pi gacchatyānandamūrcchatām |
mānavṛtteḥ pragalbhāpi tridhā dhīrādibhedataḥ ||1.103||
udāste surate dhīrā sāvahitthā ca sādarā |
santarjya niṣṭhuraṃ roṣādadhīrā tāḍayetpriyam ||1.104||
dhīrādhīraguṇopetā dhīrādhīreti kathyate |
dvedhā jyeṣṭhā kaniṣṭheti madhyā prauḍhāpi tādṛśī ||1.105||
dhīrādhīrādibhedena madhyāprauḍhe tridhā tridhā |
jyeṣṭhākaniṣṭḥābhedena tāḥ pratyekaṃ dvidhā dvidhā ||1.106||
mugdhā tvekavidhā caivaṃ sā trayodaśadhoditā |
anyāpi dvividhā kanyā paroḍhā ceti bhedataḥ ||1.107||
tatra kanyā tvanūḍhā syātsalajjā pitṛpālitā |
sakhīkeliṣu visrabdhā prāyo mugdhāguṇānvitā ||1.108||
pradhānamapradhānaṃ vā nāṭakādāviyaṃ bhavet |
mālatīmādhave lakṣye mālatīmadayantike ||1.109||
paroḍhā tu pareṇoḍhāpyanyasambhogalālasā |
lakṣyā kṣudraprabandhe sā saptaśatyādike budhaiḥ ||1.110||
sādhāraṇastrī gaṇikā kalāprāgalbhyadhārṣṭyayuk |
eṣā syāddvividhā raktā viraktā ceti bhedataḥ ||1.111||
tatra raktā tu varṇyā syādaprādhānyena nāṭake |
agnimitrasya vijñeyā yathā rājña irāvatī ||1.112||
pradhānamapradhānaṃ vā nāṭaketararūpake |
sā ceddivyā nāṭake tu prādhānyenaiva varṇyate ||1.113||
viraktā tu prahasanaprabhṛtiṣveva varṇyate |
tasyā dhauryaprabhṛtayo guāṣtadupayoginaḥ ||1.114||
channakāmān ratārthājñān bālapāṣaṇḍaṣaṇḍakān |
rakteva rañjayedibhyānniḥsvānmātrā vivāsayet ||1.115||
atra kecidāhuḥ
gaṇikāyā nānurāgo guṇavatyapi nāyake |
rasābhāsaprasaṅgaḥ syādaraktāyāśca varṇane ||

ataśca nāṭakādau tu varṇyā sā na bhavediti |

tathā cāhuḥ [śṛ.ti. 1.62,64}
sāmānyā vanitā veśyā sā dravyaṃ paramicchatā |
guṇahīne ca na dveṣo nānurāgo guṇinyapi |
śṛṅgārābhāsa etāsu na śṛṅgāraḥ kadācana ||iti||

tanmataṃ nānumanute dhīmān śrīsiṃhabhūpatiḥ ||1.116||
bhāvānubandhābhāve ca nāyikātvaparāhateḥ |
tasyāḥ prakaraṇādau ca nāyikātvavidhānataḥ ||1.117||
anāyikāvarṇane tu rasābhāsaprasaṅgataḥ |
tathā prakaraṇādīnāmarasāśrayatāgateḥ ||1.118||
rasāśrayaṃ tu daśadhetyādiśāstravirodhataḥ |
tasmātsādhāraṇastrīṇāṃ guṇaśālini nāyake ||1.119||
bhāvānubandhaḥ syādeva rudraṭasyāpi bhāṣaṇāt |
udāttādibhidāṃ kecitsarvāsāmapi manvate ||1.120||
tāstu prāyeṇa dṛśyante sarvatra vyavahārataḥ |
prathamaṃ proṣitapatikā vāskasajjā tataśca virahotkā ||1.121||
atha khaṇḍitā matā syātkalahāntaritābhisārikā caiva |
kathitā ca vipralabdhā svādhīnapatistathā cānyā ||1.122||
śṛṅgārakṛtāvasthābhedāttāścāṣṭadhā bhinnāḥ |
dūradeśaṃ gate kānte bhavetproṣitabhartṛkā ||1.123||
asyāstu jāgaraḥ kārśyaṃ nimittādivilokanam |
mālinyamanavasthānaṃ prāyaḥ śayyāniveṣaṇam ||1.124||
jāḍyacintāprabhṛtayo vikriyāḥ kathitā budhaiḥ |
bharatādayairabhidadhe strīṇāṃ vārastu vāsakaḥ ||1.125||
svavāsakavaśātkānte sameṣyati gṛhāntaram |
sajjīkaroti cātmānaṃ yā sā vāsakasajjikā ||1.126||
asyāstu ceṣṭāḥ samparkamanorathavicintanam |
sakhīvinodo hṛllekho muhurdūtinirīkṣaṇam ||1.127||
priyābhigamanamārgābhivīkṣaṇapramukhā matāḥ |
anāgasi priyatame cirayatyutsukā tu yā ||1.128||
virahotkaṇṭhitā bhāvavedibhiḥ sā samīritā |
asyāstu ceṣṭā hṛttāpo vepathuścāṅgasādanam ||1.129||
aratirbāṣpamokṣaśca svāvasthākathanādayaḥ |
ullaṅghya samayaṃ yasyāḥ preyānanyopabhogavān ||1.130||
bhogalakṣmāñcitaḥ prātarāgacchetsā hi khaṇḍitā |
asyāstu cintā niḥśvāsastūṣṇīṃbhāvo' śrumocanam ||1.131||
khedabhrāntyasphuṭālāpā ityādyā vikriyā matāḥ |
yā sakhīnāṃ puraḥ pādapatitaṃ vallabhaṃ ruṣā ||1.132||
nirasya paścāttapati kalahāntaritā hi sā |
asyāstu bhrāntisaṃlāpau moho niḥśvasitaṃ jvaraḥ ||1.133||
muhuḥ pralāpa ityādyā iṣṭāśceṣṭā manīṣibhiḥ |
madanānalasantaptā yābhisārayati priyam ||1.134||
jyotsnātāmasvinī yānayogyāmbaravibhūṣaṇā |
svayaṃ vābhisaredyā tu sā bhavedabhisārikā ||1.135||
asyāḥ santāpacintādyā vikriyāstu yathocitam |
kāntābhisaraṇae svīyā lajjānāśādiśaṅkayā ||1.136||
vyāghrahuṅkārasantrastamṛgaśāvavilocanā |
nīlyādiraktavasanaracitāṅgāvaguṇṭhanā ||1.137||
svāṅge vilīnāvayavā niḥśabdaṃ pādacāriṇī |
susnigdhaikasakhīmātrayuktā yāti samutsukā ||1.138||
mṛṣā priye tu nidrāṇe pārśve tiṣṭhati niścalā |
garvātirekanibhṛtā śītaiḥ sragdāmacandanaiḥ ||1.139||
bhāvajñā bodhayatyenaṃ tadbhāvāvekṣaṇotsukā |
svīyāvatkanyakā jñeyā kāntābhisaraṇakrame ||1.140||
veśyābhisārikā tveti hṛṣṭā vaiśikanāyakam |
āvirbhūtasmitamukhī madaghūrṇitalocanā ||1.141||
anuliptākhilāṅgī ca vicitrābharaṇānvitā |
snehāṅkuritaromāñcasphuṭībhūtamanobhavā ||1.142||
saṃveṣṭitā parijanairbhogopakaraṇānvitaiḥ |
raśanārāvamādhuryadīpitānaṅgavaibhavā ||1.143||
caraṇāmbujasaṃlagnamaṇimañjīramañjulā |
eṣā ca mṛdusaṃsparśaiḥ keśakaṇḍūyanādibhiḥ ||1.144||
prabodhayati tadbodhe praṇayātkupitekṣaṇā |
bāhuvikṣepalulitasrastadhammillamallikā ||1.145||
calitabhrūvikārādivilāsalalitekṣaṇā |
maireyāviratāsvādamadaskhalitajalpitā ||1.146||
preṣyābhiyāti dayitaṃ ceṭībhiḥ saha garvitā |
priyaṃ kaṅkaṇanikvāṇamañjuvyajanavījanaiḥ ||1.147||
vibodhya nirbhartsayati nāsābhaṅgapuraḥsaram |
kṛtvā saṅketamaprāpte daivādvyathitā tu yā ||1.148||
vipralabdheti sā proktā budhairasyāstu vikriyā |
nirvedacintākhedāśrumūrcchāniḥśvasanādayaḥ ||1.149||
svāyattāsannapatikā hṛṣṭā svādhīnavallabhā |
asyāstu ceṣṭāḥ kathitāḥ smarapūjāmahotsavaḥ ||1.150||
vanakelijalakrīḍākusumāpacayādayaḥ |
uttamā madhyamā nīcetyevaṃ sarvāḥ striyastridhā ||1.151||
abhijātairbhogatṛptairguṇibhiryā ca kāmyate |
gṛhṇāti kāraṇe kopamanunītā prasīdati ||1.152||
vidadhatyapriyaṃ patyau svayamācarati priyam |
vallabhe sāparādhe' pi tūṣṇīṃ tiṣṭhati sottamā ||1.153||
puṃsaḥ svayaṃ kāmayate kāmyate yā ca tairvadhūḥ |
sakrodhe krudhyati muhuḥ sānṛte' nṛtavādinī ||1.154||
sāpakāre' pakartrī syātsnigdhe snihyati vallabhe |
evamādiguṇopetā madhyamā sā prakīrtitā ||1.155||
akasmātkupyati ruṣaṃ prārthitāpi na muñcati |
surūpaṃ vā kurūpaṃ vā guṇavantamathāguṇam ||1.156||
sthaviraṃ taruṇaṃ vāpi yā vā kāmayate muhuḥ |
īrṣyākopavivādeṣu niyatā sādhamā smṛtā ||1.157||
svīyā trayodaśavidhā vividhā ca varāṅganā |
vaiśikaivaṃ ṣoḍaśadhā tāścāvasthābhiraṣṭabhiḥ ||1.158||
ekaikamaṣṭadhā tāsāmuttamādiprabhedataḥ |
traividhyamevaṃ sacaturaśītistriśatī bhavet ||1.159||
avasthātrayameveti kecidāhuḥ parastriyāḥ |
āsāṃ dūtyaḥ sakhī ceṭī liṅginī prativeśinī ||1.160||
dhātreyī śilpakārī ca kumārī kathinī tathā |
kārurvipraśnikā ceti netṛmitraguṇānvitāḥ ||1.161||
uddīpanaṃ caturdhā syādālambanasamāśrayam |
guṇaceṣṭālaṅkṛtayastaṭasthāśceti bhedataḥ ||1.162||
yauvanaṃ rūpalāvaṇye saundaryamabhirūpatā |
mārdavaṃ saukumāryaṃ cetyālambanagatā guṇāḥ ||1.163||
sarvāsāmapi nārīṇāṃ yauvanaṃ tu caturvidham |
pratiyauvanametāsāṃ ceṣṭitāni pṛthakpṛthak ||1.164||
īṣaccapalanetrāntaṃ smarasmeramukhāmbujam |
sagarvajarajogaṇḍamasamagrāruṇādharam ||1.165||
lāvaṇyodbhedaramyāṅgaṃ vilasadbhāvasaurabham |
unmīlitāṅkurakucamasphuṭāṅgakasandhikam ||1.166||
prathamaṃ yauvanaṃ tatra vartamānā mṛgekṣaṇā |
apekṣate mṛdusparśaṃ sahate noddhatāṃ ratim ||1.167||
sakhīkeliratā svāṅgasaṃskārakalitādarā |
na kopaharṣau bhajate sapatnīdarśanādiṣu ||1.168||
nātirajyati kāntasya saṅgame kiṃ tu lajjate |
stanau pīnau tanurmadhyaḥ pāṇipādasya raktimā ||1.169||
ūrū karikarākārāvaṅgaṃ vyaktāṅgasandhikam |
nitambo vipulo nābhirgabhīrā jaghanaṃ ghanam ||1.170||
vyaktā romāvalī snaigdhyamaṅgakeśaradākṣiṣu |
dvitīyayauvane tena kalitā vāmalocanā ||1.171||
sakhīṣu svāśayajñāsu snigdhā prāyeṇa māninī |
na prasīdatyanunaye sapatnīṣvabhyasūyinī ||1.172||
nāparādhān viṣahate praṇayerṣyākaṣāyitā |
ratikeliṣvanibhṛtā ceṣṭate garvitā rahaḥ ||1.173||
asnigdhatā nayanayorgaṇḍayormlānakāntitā |
vicchāyatā kharasparśo' pyaṅgānāṃ ślathatā manāk ||1.174||
adhare masṛṇo rāgastṛtīye yauvane bhavet |
tatra strīṇāmiyaṃ ceṣṭā ratitantravidagdhatā ||1.175||
vallabhasyāparityāgastadākarṣaṇakauśalam |
anādaro' parādheṣu sapatnīṣvapyamatsaraḥ ||1.176||
jarjaratvaṃ stanaśroṇigaṇḍorujaghanādiṣu |
nirmāṃsatā ca bhavati caturthe yauvane striyāḥ ||1.177||
tatra ceṣṭā ratividhāvanutsāho' samarthatā |
sapatnīṣvānukūlyaṃ ca kāntenāvirahasthitiḥ ||1.178||
tatra śṛṅgārayogyatvaṃ sarasāhlādakāraṇam |
ādyadvitīyayoreva na tṛtīyacaturthayoḥ ||1.179||
aṅgānyabhūṣitānyeva prakṣepādyairvibhūṣaṇaiḥ |
yena bhūṣitavadbhāti tadrūpamiti kathyate ||1.180||
muktāphaleṣu chāyāyāstaralatvamivāntarā |
pratibhāti yadaṅgeṣu lāvaṇyaṃ tadihocyate ||1.181||
aṅgapratyāngakānāṃ yaḥ sanniveśo yathocitam |
susliṣṭasandhibandhaḥ syāttatsaundaryamitīryate ||1.182||
yadātmīyaguṇotkarṣairvastvanyannikaṭasthitam |
sārūpyaṃ nayati prājñairābhirūpyaṃ taducyate ||1.183||
spṛṣṭaṃ yatrāṅgamaspṛṣṭamiva syānmārdavaṃ hi tat |
yā sparśāsahatāṅgeṣu komalasyāpi vastunaḥ ||1.184||
tatsaukumāryaṃ tredhā syānmukhyamadhyādhamakramāt |
aṅgaṃ puṣpādisaṃsparśāsahaṃ yena taduttamam ||1.185||
na saheta karasparśaṃ yenāṅgaṃ madhyamaṃ hi tat |
yenāṅgamātapādīnāmasahaṃ tadihādhamam ||1.186||
caturdhālaṅkṛtirvāsobhūṣāmālyānulepanaiḥ |
taṭasthāścandrikā dhārāgṛhacandrodayāvapi ||1.187||
kokilālāpamākandamandamārutaṣaṭpadāḥ |
latāmaṇḍapabhūgehadīrghikājaladāravāḥ ||1.188||
prāsādagarbhasaṅgītakrīḍādrisaridādayaḥ |
evamūhyā yathā kālamupabhogopayoginaḥ ||1.189||
[ālambanagatāśceṣṭā anubhāvā vivakṣitāḥ |]
bhāvaṃ manogataṃ sākṣātsvahetuṃ vyañjayanti ye |
te' nubhāvā iti khyātā bhrūkṣepasmitādayaḥ ||1.190||
te caturdhā cittagātravāgbuddhyārambhasambhavāḥ |
tatra ca bhāvo hāvo helā śobhā kāntidīptī ca ||1.191||
prāgalbhyaṃ mādhuryaṃ dhairyaudāryaṃ ca cittajā bhāvāḥ |
nirvikārasya cittasya bhāvaḥ syādādivikriyā ||1.192||
grīvārecakasaṃyukto bhrūnetrādivilāsakṛt |
bhāva īṣatprakāśo yaḥ sa hāva iti kathyate ||1.193||
nānāvikāraiḥ suvyaktaḥ śṛṅgārākṛtisūcakaiḥ |
hāva eva bhaveddhelā lalitābhinayātmikā ||1.194||
sā śobhā rūpabhogādyairyatsyādaṅgavibhūṣaṇam |
śobhaiva kāntirākhyātā manmathāpyāyanojjvalā ||1.195||
kāntireva vayobhogadeśakālaguṇādibhiḥ ||
uddīpitātivistāraṃ yātā ceddīptirucyate ||1.196||
niḥśaṅkatvaṃ prayogeṣu prāgalbhyaṃ parikīrtyate |
mādhuryaṃ nāma ceṣṭānāṃ sarvāvasthāsu mārdavam ||1.197||
sthirā cittonnatiryā tu taddhairyamiti saṃjñitam |
audāryaṃ vinayaṃ prāhuḥ sarvāvasthānugaṃ budhāḥ ||1.198||
līlā vilāso vicchittirvibhramaḥ kilakiñcitam |
moṭṭāyitaṃ kuṭṭamitaṃ bibboko lalitaṃ tathā ||1.199||
vihṛtaṃ ceti vijñeyā yoṣitāṃ daśa gātrajāḥ |
priyānukaraṇaṃ yattu madhurālāpapūrvakaiḥ ||1.200||
ceṣṭitairgatibhirvā syātsā līleti nigadyate |
priyasamprāptisamaye bhrūnetrānanakarmaṇām ||1.201||
tātkāliko viśeṣo yaḥ sa vilāsa itīritaḥ |
ākalpakalpanālpāpi vicchittiratikāntikṛt ||1.202||
priyāgamanavelāyāṃ madanāveśasambhramāt |
vibhramo' ṅgadahārādibhūṣāsthānaviparyayaḥ ||1.203||
śokaroṣāśruharṣādeḥ saṅkaraḥ kilakiñcitam |
svābhilāṣaprakaṭaṃ moṭṭāyitamitīritam ||1.204||
keśādharādigrahaṇe modamāne' pi mānase |
duḥkhiteva bahiḥ kupyedyatra kuṭṭamitaṃ hi tat ||1.205||
iṣṭe' pyanādaro garvānmānādbibboka īritaḥ |
vinyāsabhaṅgiraṅgānāṃ bhrūvilāsamanoharāḥ ||1.206||
sukumārā bhavedyatra lalitaṃ tadudīritam |
īrṣyayā mānalajjābhyāṃ na dattaṃ yogyamuttaram ||1.207||
kriyayā vyajyate yatra vihṛtaṃ tadudīritam |
itthaṃ śrīsiṃhabhūpena sattvālaṅkāraśālinā ||1.208||
kathitāḥ sattvajāḥ strīṇāmalaṅkārāstu viṃśatiḥ |
sattvāddaśaiva bhāvādyā jātā līlādayastu na ||1.209||
ato hi viṃśatirbhāvāḥ sāttvikā iti nocitam |
yujyate sāttvikatvaṃ ca bhāvādisahacāriṇaḥ ||1.210||
līlādidaśakasyāpi chatrinyāyabalātsphuṭam |
bhojena krīḍitaṃ kelirityanyau gātrajau smṛtau ||1.211||
ato viṃśatirityatra saṅkhyeyaṃ nopapadyate |
atrocyate bhāvatattvavedinā siṃhabhūbhujā ||1.212||
ādyaḥ prāgeva bhāvādisamutpatteśca śaiśave |
kanyāvinodamātratvādanubhāveṣu neṣyate ||1.213||
premavisrambhamātratvānnānyasyāpyanubhāvatā |
ato viṃśatirityeṣā saṅkhyā saṅkhyāvatāṃ matā ||1.214||
śobhā vilāso mādhuryaṃ dhairyaṃ gāmbhīryameva ca |
lalitaudāryatejāṃsi sattvabhedāstu pauruṣāḥ ||1.215||
nīce dayādhike spardhā śauryotsāhau ca dakṣatā |
yatra prakaṭatāṃ yānti sā śobheti prakīrtitā ||1.216||
vṛṣabhasyeva gambhīrā gatirdhīraṃ ca darśanam |
sasmitaṃ ca vaco yatra sa vilāsa itīritaḥ ||1.217||
tanmādhuryaṃ yatra ceṣṭādṛṣṭyādeḥ spṛhaṇīyatā |
śṛṅgārapracurā ceṣṭā yatra tallalitaṃ bhavet ||1.218||
atra gāmbhīryadhairye dve cittaje gātrajāḥ pare |
eke sādhāraṇānetānmenire cittagātrayoḥ ||1.219||
ālāpaśca vilāpaśca saṃlāpaśca pralāpakaḥ |
anulāpāpalāpau ca sandeśaścātideśakaḥ ||1.220||
nirdeśaścopadeśaścāpadeśo vyapadeśakaḥ |
evaṃ dvādaśadhā proktā kīrtitā vāgārambhā vicakṣaṇaiḥ ||1.221||
tatrālāpaḥ priyoktiḥ syādvilāpo duḥkhajaṃ vacaḥ |
uktipratyuktimadvākyaṃ saṃlāpa iti kīrtitam ||1.222||
vyarthālāpaḥ pralāpaḥ syādanulāpo muhurvacaḥ |
apalāpastu pūrvoktasyānyathā yojanaṃ bhavet ||1.223||
sandeśastu proṣitasya svavārtāpreṣaṇaṃ bhavet |
so' tideśo maduktāni taduktānīti yadvacaḥ ||1.224||
nirdeśastu bhavetso' yamahamityādibhāṣaṇam |
yatra śikṣārthavacanamupadeśaḥ sa ucyate ||1.225||
anyārthakathanaṃ yatra so' padeśa itīritaḥ |
vyājenātmābhilāṣoktiryatrāyaṃ vyapadeśakaḥ ||1.226||
buddhyārambhāstathā proktā rītivṛttipravṛttayaḥ |
rītiḥ syātpadavinyāsabhaṅgī sā tu tridhā matā ||1.227||
komalā kaṭhinā miśrā ceti syāttatra komalā |
dvitīyaturyavarṇairyā svalpairvargeṣu nirmitā ||1.228||
alpaprāṇākṣaraprāyā daśaprāṇasamanvitā |
samāsarahitā svalpaiḥ samāsairvā vibhūṣitā ||1.229||
vidarbhajanahṛdyatvātsā vaidarbhīti kathyate |
śleṣaḥ prasādaḥ samatā mādhuryaṃ sukumāratā ||1.230||
arthavyaktirudāratvamojaḥ kāntisamādhayaḥ |
ete vaidarbhamārgasya prāṇā daśa guṇāḥ smṛtāḥ ||1.231||
kevalālpaprāṇavarṇapadasandarbhalakṣaṇam |
śaithilyaṃ yatra na spṛṣṭaṃ sa śleṣaḥ samudāhṛtaḥ ||1.232||
prasiddhārthapadatvaṃ yatsa prasādo nigadyate |
bandhavaiṣamyarāhityaṃ samatā padagumphane ||1.233||
bandho mṛduḥ sphuṭo miśra iti tredhā sa nigadyate |
tanmādhuryaṃ bhavedyatra śabde' rthe ca sphuṭo rasaḥ ||1.234||
yadaniṣṭhuravarṇatvaṃ saukumāryaṃ taducyate |
śrūyamāṇasya vākyasya vinā śabdāntaraspṛhām ||1.235||
arthāvagamakatvaṃ yadarthavyaktiriyaṃ matā |
ukte vākye guṇotkarṣapratibhānamudāratā ||1.236||
samāsabahulatvaṃ yattadojaḥ iti gīyate |
lokasthitimanullaṅghya hṛdyārthapratipādanam ||1.237||
kāntiḥ syāddvividhā khyātā vārtāyāṃ varṇanāsu ca |
samādhiḥ so' nyadharmāṇāṃ yadanyatrādhiropaṇam ||1.238||
atidīrghasamāsayutā bahulairvarṇairyutā mahāprāṇaiḥ |
kaṭhinā sā gauḍīyetyuktā taddeśabudhamanojñatvāt ||1.239||
yatrobhayaguṇagrāmasaṃniveśastulādhṛtaḥ |
sā miśrā saiva pāñcālītyuktā taddeśajapriyā ||1.240||
āndhrī lāṭī ca saurāṣṭrītyādayo miśrarītayaḥ |
santi tattaddeśavidvatpriyamiśraṇabhedataḥ ||1.241||
ta eva padasaṅghātāstā evārthavibhūtayaḥ |
tathāpi navyaṃ bhavati kāvyaṃ grathanakauśalāt ||1.242||
tāsāṃ granthagaḍutvena lakṣaṇaṃ nocyate mayā |
bhojādigranthabandheṣu tadākāṅkṣibhirīkṣyatām ||1.243||
bhāratī sātvatī caiva kaiśikyārabhaṭīti ca |
catasro vṛttayastāsāmutpattirvakṣyate sphuṭam ||1.244||
jagatyekārṇave jāte bhagavānavyayaḥ pumān |
bhogibhogamadhiṣṭhāya yoganidrāparo' bhavat ||1.245||
tadā vīryamadonmattau daityendrau madhukaiṭabhau |
tarasā devadeveśamāgatau raṇakāṅkṣiṇau ||1.246||
vividhaiḥ paruṣairvākyairadhikṣepavidhāyinau |
muṣṭijānuprahāraiśca yodhayāmāsaturharim ||1.247||
tannābhikamalotpannaḥ prajāpatirabhāṣata |
kimetadbhāratīvṛttiradhunāpi pravartate ||1.248||
tadimau naya durdharṣau nidhanaṃ tvarayā vibho |
iti tasya vacaḥ śrutvā nijagāda janārdanaḥ ||1.249||
idaṃ kāvyakriyāhetorbhāratī nirmitā dhruvam |
bhāṣaṇādvākyabāhulyādbhāratīyaṃ bhaviṣyati ||1.250||
adhunaiva nihanmyetāvityābhāṣya vaco hariḥ |
nirmalairnirvikāraiśca sāṅgahārairmanoharaiḥ ||1.251||
aṅgaistau yodhayāmāsa daityendrau yuddhaśālinau |
bhūmisthānakasaṃyogaiḥ padakṣepaistathā hareḥ ||1.252||
bhūmestadābhavadbhārastadvaśādapi bhāratī |
valgitaiḥ śārṅgiṇastatra dīptaiḥ sambhramavarjitaiḥ ||1.253||
sattvādhikairbāhudaṇḍaiḥ sātvatvī vṛttirudgatā |
vicitrairaṅgahāraiśca helayā sa tadā hariḥ ||1.254||
yattau babandha keśeṣu jātā sā kaiśikī tataḥ |
sasaṃrambhaiḥ savegaiśca citracārīsamutthitaiḥ ||1.255||
niyuddhakaraṇairjātā citrairārabhaṭī tataḥ |
yasmāccitrairaṅgahāraiḥ kṛtaṃ dānavamardanam ||1.256||
tasmādabjabhuvā loke niyuddhasamayaḥ kṛtaḥ |
yaḥ śastrāstrādimokṣeṣu nyāyaḥ sa pāribhāṣitaḥ ||1.257||
nāṭyakāvyakriyāyoge rasabhāvasamāśritaḥ |
sa eva samayo dhātrā vṛttirityeva saṃjñitaḥ ||1.258||
hariṇā tena yadvastu valigitairyādṛśaṃ kṛtam |
tadvadeva kṛtā vṛttirdhātrā tasyāṅgasambhavā ||1.259||
ṛgvedācca yajurvedātsāmavedādatharvaṇaḥ |
bhāratyādyā kramājjātā ityanye tu pracakṣate ||1.260||
prayuktatvena bharatairbhāratīti nigadyate |
prastāvanopayogitvātsāṅgaṃ tatraiva lakṣyate ||1.261||
sāttvikena guṇenātityāgaśauryādinā yutā |
harṣapradhānā santyaktaśokabhāvā ca yā bhavet ||1.262||
sātvatī nāma sā vṛttiḥ proktā lakṣaṇakovidaiḥ |
aṅgānyasyāstu catvāri saṃlāpottāpakāvapi ||1.263||
saṅghātyaḥ parivartaścetyeṣāṃ lakṣaṇamucyate |
īrṣyākrodhādibhirbhāvai rasairvīrādbhutādibhiḥ ||1.264||
parasparaṃ gabhīroktiḥ saṃlāpa iti śabdyate |
preraṇaṃ yatparasyādau yuddhāyotthāpakastu saḥ ||1.265||
mantraśaktyārthaśaktyā vā daivaśaktyātha pauruṣāt |
saṅghasya bhedanaṃ yattu saṅghātyaḥ sa udāhṛtaḥ ||1.266||
pūrvodyuktasya kāryasya parityāgena yadbhavet |
kāryāntarasvīkaraṇaṃ jñeyaḥ sa parivartakaḥ ||1.267||
nṛtyagītavilāsādimṛduśṛṅgāraceṣṭitaiḥ |
samanvitā bhavedvṛttiḥ kaiśikī ślakṣṇabhūṣaṇā ||1.268||
aṅgānyasyāstu catvāri narma tatpūrvakā ime |
sphañjasphoṭau ca garbhaśca teṣāṃ lakṣaṇamucyate ||1.269||
śṛṅgārarasabhūyiṣṭhaḥ priyacittānurañjakaḥ |
agrāmyaḥ parihāsastu narma syāttattridhā matam ||1.270||
śṛṅgārahāsyajaṃ śuddhahāsyajaṃ bhayahāsyajam |
śṛṅgārahāsyajaṃ narma trividhaṃ parikīrtitam ||1.271||
sambhogecchāprakaṭanādanurāganiveśanāt |
tathā kṛtāparādhasya priyasya pratibhedanāt ||1.272||
sambhogecchāprakaṭanaṃ tridhā vāgveṣaceṣṭitaiḥ |
anurāgaprakāśo' pi bhogecchānarmavattridhā ||1.273||
priyāparādhanirbhedo' pyuktastredhā tathā budhaiḥ |
śuddhahāsyajamapyuktaṃ tadvadeva tridhā budhaiḥ ||1.274||
hāsyādbhayena janitājjanitaṃ bhayahāsyajam |
taddvidhā mukhamaṅgaṃ tu taddvayaṃ pūrvavattridhā ||1.275||
agrāmyanarmanirmāṇavedinā siṃhabhūbhujā |
narmāṣṭādaśadhā bhinnameva sphuṭamudāhṛtam ||1.276||
narmasphañjaḥ sukhodyogo bhayānto navasaṅgame |
narmasphoṭastu bhāvāṃśaiḥ sūcito' lparaso bhavet ||1.277||
anyaistvakāṇḍe sambhogaviccheda iti gīyate |
neturvā nāyikāyā vā vyāpāraḥ svārthasiddhaye ||1.278||
pracchādanaparo yastu narmagarbhaḥ sa kīrtitaḥ |
pūrvasthito vipadyeta nāyako yatra cāparastiṣṭhet ||1.279||
tamapīha narmagarbhaṃ pravadati bharato hi nāṭyavedaguruḥ |
māyendrajālapracurāṃ citrayuddhakriyāmayīm ||1.280||
chedyairbhedyaiḥ plutairyuktāṃ vṛttimārabhaṭīṃ viduḥ |
aṅgānyasyāstu catvāri saṅkṣiptiravapātanam ||1.281||
vastūtthāpanasampheṭāviti pūrve babhāṣire |
saṅkṣiptavastuviṣayā yā māyāśilpayojitā ||1.282||
sā saṅkṣiptiriti proktā bharatena mahātmanā |
vadantyanye tu tāṃ neturavasthāntarasaṅgatim ||1.283||
parivartakabhedatvāttadupekṣāmahe vayam |
vibhrāntiravapātaḥ syātpraveśadravavidravaiḥ ||1.284||
tadvastūtthāpanaṃ yattu vastu māyopakalpitam |
sampheṭastu samāghātaḥ kruddhasaṃrabdhayordvayoḥ ||1.285||
āsāṃ ca madhye vṛttīnāṃ śabdavṛttistu bhāratī |
tisro' rthavṛttayaḥ śeṣāstaccatasro hi vṛttayaḥ ||1.286||
anye tu miśraṇādāsāṃ miśrāṃ vṛttiṃ ca pañcamīm |
aśeṣarasasāmānyāṃ manyante lakṣayanti ca ||1.287||
kaiśikī syāttu śṛṅgāre rase vīre tu sātvatī |
radurabībhatsayorvṛttirniyatārabhaṭī punaḥ ||1.288||
śṛṅgārādiṣu sarveṣu raseṣviṣṭaiva bhāratī |
kecittu tamimaṃ ślokaṃ bhāratīyaṃ niyāmakam ||1.289||
prāyikābhiprāyatayā vyācakṣāṇā vicakṣaṇāḥ |
āsāṃ raseṣu vṛttīnāṃ niyamaṃ nānumanvate ||1.290||
vicārasundaro naiṣa mārgaḥ syādityudāsmahe |
kaiśikīvṛttibhedānāṃ narmādīnāṃ prakalpanam ||1.291||
yatra karuṇamāśritya rasābhāsatvakāraṇam |
rasābhāsaprakaraṇe vakṣyate tadidaṃ sphuṭam ||1.292||
tattannyāyapravīṇena nyāyamārgānuvartinā |
darśitaṃ siṃhabhūpena spaṣṭaṃ vṛtticatuṣṭayam ||1.293||
tattaddeśocitā bhāṣā kriyā veṣā pravṛttayaḥ |
tatra bhāṣā dvidhā bhāṣā vibhāṣā ceti bhedataḥ ||1.294||
tatra bhāṣā saptavidhā prācyāvantyā ca māgadhī |
bāhlīkā dākṣiṇātyā ca śaurasenī ca mālavī ||1.295||
saptadhā syādvibhāṣādi śabaradramilāndhrajāḥ |
śakārābhīracaṇḍālavanecarabhavā iti ||1.296||
bhāṣāvibhāṣāḥ santyanyāstattaddeśajanocitāḥ |
tāsāmanupayogitvānnātra lakṣaṇamucyate ||1.297||
tattaddeśocitā veṣāḥ kriyāścātisphuṭāntarāh |
anyeṣāṃ sukhaduḥkhādibhāveṣu kṛtabhāvanam ||1.298||
ānukūlyena yaccittaṃ bhāvakānāṃ pravartate |
sattvaṃ taditi vijñeyaṃ prājñaiḥ sattvodbhavānimān ||1.299||
sāttvikā iti jānanti bharatādimaharṣayaḥ |
sarveṣāmapi bhāvānāṃ yaiḥ svasattvaṃ hi bhāvyate ||1.300||
te bhāvā bhāvatattvajñaiḥ sāttvikā samudīritāḥ |
te stambhasvedaromāñcāḥ svarabhedaśca vepathuḥ ||1.301||
vaivarṇyamaśrupralayāvityaṣṭau parikīrtitāḥ |
stambho harṣabhayāmarṣaviṣādādbhutasambhavaḥ ||1.302||
anubhāvā bhavantyete stambhasya munisaṃmatāḥ |
saṃjñāvirahitatvaṃ ca śūnyatā niṣprakampatā ||1.303||
nidāghaharṣavyāyāmaśramakrodhabhayādibhiḥ |
svedaḥ sañjāyate tatra tvanubhāvā bhavantyamī ||1.304||
svedāpanayavātecchāvyajanagrahaṇādayaḥ |
romāñco vismayotsāhaharṣādyaistatra vikriyāḥ ||1.305||
romodgamollukasanagātrasaṃsparśanādayaḥ |
vaisvaryaṃ sukhaduḥkhādyaistatra syurgadgadādayaḥ ||1.306||
vepathurharṣasantrāsajarākrodhādibhirbhavet |
tatrānubhāvāḥ sphuraṇagātrakampādayo matāḥ ||1.307||
viṣādātaparoṣādyairvaivarṇyamupajāyate |
mukhavarṇaparāvṛttikārśyādyāstatra vikriyāḥ ||1.308||
viṣādaroṣasantoṣādhūmādyairaśru tatkriyāḥ |
bāṣpabinduparikṣepanetrasaṃmārjanādayaḥ ||1.309||
pralayo duḥkhadhātādyaiśceṣṭā tatra visaṃjñatā |
sarve' pi sattvamūlatvādbhāvā yadyapi sāttvikāḥ ||1.310||
tathāpyamīṣāṃ sattvaikamūlatvātsāttvikaprathā |
anubhāvāśca kathyante bhāvasaṃsūcanādamī ||1.311||
evaṃ dvairūpyameteṣāṃ kathitaṃ bhāvakovidaiḥ |
anubhāvaikanidhinā sukhānubhavaśālinā |
śrīsiṃhabhūbhujā sāṅgamanubhāvā nirūpitāḥ ||1.312||

asmatkalpalatādalāni gilati tvatkāmagaurvāryatāṃ
maccintāmaṇivedibhiḥ pariṇameddūrānnayoccairgajam |
ityārūḍhavitardikāḥ pratipathaṃ jalpanti bhūdevatāḥ
siṃhakṣmābhuji kalpavṛkṣasurabhīhastyādidānodyate ||1.313||

rakṣāyāṃ rākṣasāriṃ prabalavimatavidrāvaṇe vīrabhadraṃ
kāruṇye rāmabhadraṃ bhujabalavibhavārohaṇe rauhiṇeyam |
pāñcālaṃ cañcalākṣīparicaraṇavidhau pūrṇacandraṃ prasāde
kandarparūpadarpe tulayati nitarāṃ siṃhabhūpālacandraḥ ||1.314||


iti śrīmadāndhramaṇḍalādhīśvarapratigaṇḍabhairavaśrīmadanapotanarendranandanabhujabalabhīmaśrīsiṃhabhūpālaviracite rasārṇavasudhākaranāmni nāṭyālaṅkāraśāstre rañjakollāso nāma
prathamo vilāsaḥ
||2.1||

o)0(o
ḥ
(2)
dvitīyo vilāsaḥ
rasikollāsaḥ

kalyāṇadāyi bhavatāṃ bhavedbhavyaguṇākaram |
kamalākucakāleyavyañjitoraḥsthalaṃ mahaḥ ||3.1||
cidacitkṣemakāriṇyai namaḥ śrīparṇajādibhiḥ |
vandyāyai vārdhinandinyai karāgrasthapayoruhe ||2.2||
vyabhī ityupasargau dvau viśeṣābhimukhatvayoḥ |
viśeṣeṇābhimukhyena caranti sthāyinaṃ prati ||2.3||
vāgaṅgasattvasūcyā jñeyāste vyabhicāriṇaḥ |
taṃ cārayanti bhāvasya gatiṃ sañcāriṇo' pi ||2.4||
unmajjanto nimajjantaḥ sthāyinyamṛtavāridhau |
ūrmivadvardhayantyenaṃ yānti tadrūpatāṃ ca te ||2.5||
nirvedo' tha viṣādo dainyaṃ glāniśramau ca madagarvau |
śaṅkātrāsāvegā unmādāpasmṛtī tathā vyādhiḥ ||2.6||
moho mṛtirālasyaṃ jāḍyaṃ vrīḍāvahitthā ca |
smṛtiratha vitarkacintāmatidhṛtayo harṣa utsukatvaṃ ca ||2.7||
augryamarṣāsūyāścāpalyaṃ caiva nidrā ca |
suptirbodha itīme bhāvā vyabhicāriṇaḥ samākhyātāḥ ||2.8||
tattvajñānācca daurgatyāvāpado viprayogataḥ |
īrṣyāderapi saṃjātaṃ nirvedaḥ svāvamānanam [*1] ||2.9||
prārabdhakāryānirvāhādiṣṭānavāptervipattitaḥ |
aparādhaparijñānādanutāpastu yo bhavet ||2.10||
viṣādaḥ sa tridhā jyeṣṭhamadhyamādhamasaṃśrayāt |
sahāyānveṣaṇopāyacintādyā uttame matāḥ ||2.11||
anutsāhaśca vaicittyamityādyā madhyame matāḥ |
adhamasyānubhāvāḥ syurvaicitryamavalokanam ||2.12||
rodanaśvāsitadhyānamukhaśoṣādayo' pi ca |
hṛttāpadurgatatvādyairanauddhatyaṃ hi dīnatā ||2.13||
tatrānubhāvā mālinyagātrastambhādayo matāḥ |
ādhivyādhijarātṛṣṇāvyāyāmasuratādibhiḥ ||2.14||
niṣprāṇatā glāniratra kṣāmāṅgavacanakriyāḥ |
kampānutsāhavaivarṇyanayanabhramaṇādayaḥ ||2.15||
śramo mānasakhedaḥ syādadhvanṛtyaratādobhiḥ |
aṅgamardananiḥśvāsau pādasaṃvāhanaṃ tathā ||2.16||
jṛmbhaṇaṃ mandayānaṃ ca mukhanetravighūrṇanam |
sītkṛtiśceti vijñeyā anubhāvāḥ śramodbhavāḥ ||2.17||
madastvānandasaṃmohasambhedo madirākṛtaḥ |
sa tridhā taruṇo madhyo' pakṛṣṭaśceti bhedataḥ ||2.18||
dṛṣṭiḥ smerā mukhe rāgaḥ sasmitākulitaṃ vacaḥ |
lalitāviddhagatyādyāśceṣṭāḥ syustaruṇe made ||2.19||
madhyame tu made vāci skhalanaṃ ghūrṇanaṃ dṛśoḥ |
gamane vaktratā bāhvorvikṣepasrastatādayaḥ ||2.20||
apakṛṣṭe tu ceṣṭāḥ syurgatibhaṅgo visaṃjñatā |
niṣṭhīvanaṃ muhuḥ śvāso hikkā chardyādayo matāḥ ||2.21||
taruṇastūttamādīnāṃ madhyamo madhyanīcayoḥ |
apakṛṣṭastu nīcānāṃ tattanmadavivardhane ||2.22||
uttamaprakṛtiḥ śete madhyo hasati gāyati |
adhamaprakṛtirgrāmyaṃ paruṣaṃ vakti roditi ||2.23||
aiśvaryādikṛtaḥ kaiścitmāno mada itīritaḥ |
vakṣyamāṇasya garvasya bheda evetyudāsmahe ||2.24||
aiśvaryarūpatāruṇyakulavidyābalairapi |
iṣṭalābhādinānyeṣāmavajñā garva īritaḥ ||2.25||
anubhāvā bhavantyatra gurvājñādyājñāvyatikramaḥ |
anuttarapraadānaṃ ca vaimukhyaṃ bhāṣaṇe' pi ca ||2.26||
vibhramāpahnutī vākyapāruṣyamanavekṣaṇam |
avekṣaṇaṃ nijāṅgānāmaṅgabhaṅgādayo' pi ca ||2.27||
śaṅkā cauryāparādhādyaiḥ svāniṣṭotprekṣaṇaṃ matam |
tatra ceṣṭāmuhuḥ pārśvadarśanaṃ mukhaśoṣaṇam ||2.28||
avakuṇṭhanavaivarṇyakaṇṭhasādādayo' pi ca |
śaṅkā dvidyeyamātmotthā parotthā ceti bhedataḥ ||2.29||
svākāryajanitā svotthā prāyo vyaṅgyeyamiṅgitaiḥ |
iṅgitāni tu pakṣmabhrūtārakādṛṣṭivikriyāḥ ||2.30||
parotthā tu nijasyaiva parasyākāryato bhavet |
prāyeṇākāraceṣṭābhyāṃ tāmimāmanubhāvayet ||2.31||
ākāraḥ sāttvikaśceṣṭā tvaṅgapratyaṅgajāḥ kriyāḥ |
trāsastu cittacāñcalyaṃ vidyutkravyādagarjitaiḥ ||2.32||
tathā bhūtabhujaṅgādyairvijñeyāstatra vikriyāḥ |
utkampagātrasaṅkocaromāñcastambhagadgadāḥ ||2.33||
muhurnimeṣavibhrāntipārśvasthālambanādayaḥ |
cittasya sambhramo yaḥ syādāvego' yaṃ sa cāṣṭadhā ||2.34||
utpātavātavarṣāgnimattakuñjaradarśanāt |
priyāpriyaśruteścāpi śatravavyasanādapi ||2.35||
tatrautpātastu śailādikampaketūdayādayaḥ |
tajjāḥ sarvāṅgavisraṃso vaimukhyamapasarpaṇam ||2.36||
viṣādamukhavaivarṇyavismayādyāstu vikriyāḥ |
tvarayāgamanaṃ vastragrahaṇaṃ cāvakuṇṭhanam ||2.37||
netrāvamārjanādyāśca vātāvegabhavāḥ kriyāḥ |
chatragraho' ṅgasaṅkoco bāhusvastikadhāvane ||2.38||
uṣṇāśrayaṇamityādyā varṣāvegabhavāḥ kriyāḥ |
agnyāvegaḥbhavāśceṣṭā vījanaṃ cāṅgadhūnanam ||2.39||
vyatyastapadavikṣepanetrasaṅkocanādayaḥ |
āvege kuñjarodbhūte satvaraṃ cāpasarpaṇam ||2.40||
vilokanaṃ muhuḥ paścāttrāsakampādayo matāḥ |
priyaśravaṇaje hyasminabhutthānopagūhane ||2.41||
prītidānaṃ priyaṃ vākyaṃ romaharṣādayo' pi ca |
apriyaśrutije' pyasmin vilāpahparivartanam ||2.42||
ākranditaṃ ca patanaṃ parito bhramaṇādayaḥ |
ceṣṭāḥ syuḥ śātravāvege varmaśastrādidhāraṇam ||2.43||
rathavājigajārohasahasāpakramādayaḥ |
ete syuruttamādīnāmanubhāvā yathocitam ||2.44||
unmādaścittavibhrāntirviyogādiṣṭanāśataḥ |
viyogaje tu ceṣṭāḥ syurdhāvanaṃ paridevanam ||2.45||
asambaddhapralapanaṃ śayaṇaṃ sahasotthitiḥ |
acetanaiḥ sahālāpo nirnimittasmitādayaḥ ||2.46||
iṣṭanāśakṛte tvasmin bhasmādiparilepanam |
nṛtyagītādiracanā tṛṇanirmālyadhāraṇam ||2.47||
cīvarāvaraṇādīni prāguktāścāpi vikriyāḥ |
dhātuvaiṣamyadoṣeṇa bhūtāveśādinā kṛtaḥ ||2.48||
cittakṣobhastvapasmārastatra ceṣṭāḥ prakampanam |
dhāvanaṃ patanaṃ stambho bhramaṇaṃ netravikriyāḥ ||2.49||
svoṣṭhadaṃśabhujāsphoṭalālāphenādayo' pi ca |
doṣavaiṣamyajastveṣa vyādhirevetyudāsmahe ||2.50||
doṣodrekaviyogādyairsyādvyādhiratra tu |
gātrastambhaḥ ślathāṅgatvaṃ kūjanaṃ mukhakūṇanam ||2.51||
srastāṅgatākṣivikṣepaniḥśvāsādyāstu vikriyāḥ |
saśīto dāhayuktaḥ sa dvividhaḥ parikīrtitaḥ [*2] ||2.52||
hanusañcālanaṃ bāṣpaḥ sarvāṅgotkampakūjane |
jānukuñcanaromāñcamukhaśoṣādayo' pi ca ||2.53||
dāhajvare tu ceṣṭāḥ syuḥ śītamālyādikāṅkṣaṇam |
pāṇipādaparikṣepamukhaśoṣādayo' pi ca ||2.54||
āpadbhītiviyogādyairmohaścittasya mūḍhatā |
vikriyāstatra vijñeyā indriyāṇāṃ ca śūnyatā ||2.55||
niśceṣṭatāṅgabhramaṇapatanāghūrṇanādayaḥ |
vāyordhanañjayākhyasya viprayogo ya ātmanā ||2.56||
śarīrāvacchedavatā maraṇaṃ nāma tadbhavet |
etacca dvividhaṃ proktaṃ vyādhijaṃ cābhighātajam ||2.57||
ādyaṃ tvasādhyahṛcchūlaviṣūcyādisamudbhavam |
amī tatrānubhāvāḥ syuravyaktākṣarabhāṣaṇam ||2.58||
vivarṇagātratā mandaśvāsādi stambhamīlane |
hikkā parijanāpekṣāniśceṣṭendriyatādayaḥ ||2.59||
dvitīyaṃ ghātapatanadohodbandhaviṣādijam |
tatra ghātādije bhūmipatanakrandanādayaḥ ||2.60||
viṣaṃ tu vatsanābhādyamaṣṭau vegāstadudbhavāḥ |
kārṣṇyaṃ kampo dāho hikkā phenaśca kandharabhaṅgaḥ ||2.61||
jaḍatā mṛtiriti kathitā kramaśaḥ prathamādyā vegajāśceṣṭāḥ |
svabhāvaśramasauhityagarbhanirbharatādibhiḥ ||2.62||
kṛcchrātkriyonmukhatvaṃ yattadālasyamiha kriyāḥ |
aṅgabhaṅgaḥ kriyādveṣo jṛmbhaṇākṣivimardane ||2.63||
śayyāsanaikapriyatā tandrīnidrādayo' pi ca |
jāḍyamapratipattiḥ syādiṣṭāniṣṭhārthayoḥ śruteḥ ||2.64||
dṛṣṭervā virahādeśca kriyāstatrānimeṣatā |
aśrutiḥ pāravaśyaṃ ca tūṣṇīmbhāvādayo' pi ca ||2.65||
akāryakaraṇāvajñāstutinūtanasaṅgamaiḥ |
pratīkārākriyādyaiśca vrīḍatvanatidhṛṣṭatā ||2.66||
tatra ceṣṭā nigūḍhoktirādhomukhyavicintane |
anirgamo bahiḥ kvāpi dūrādevāvaguṇṭhanam ||2.67||
nakhānāṃ kṛntanaṃ bhūmilekhanaṃ caivamādayaḥ |
avahitthākāraguptirjaihmyaprābhavanītibhiḥ ||2.68||
lajjāsādhvasadākṣiṇyaprāgalbhyāpajayādibhiḥ |
anyathākathanaṃ mithyādhairyamanyatra vīkṣaṇam ||2.69||
kathābhaṅgādayo' pyasyāmanubhāvā bhavantyamī |
svāsthyacintādṛḍhābhyāsasadṛśālokanādibhiḥ ||2.70||
smṛtiḥ pūrvānubhūtārthapratītistatra vikriyāḥ |
kampanodvahane mūrdhno bhrūvikṣepādayo' pi ca ||2.71||
ūho vitarkaḥ sandehavimarṣapratyayādibhiḥ |
janito nirṇayāntaḥ syādasatyaḥ satya eva vā ||2.72||
tatrānubhāvāḥ syuramī bhrūśiraḥ ksepaṇādayaḥ |
iṣṭavastvapariprāpteraiśvaryabhraṃśanādibhiḥ ||2.73||
cintā dhyānātmikā tasyāmanubhāvā bhavantyamī |
kārśyādhomukhyasantāpaniḥśvāsocchrvasanādayaḥ ||2.74||
nānāśāstrārathamathanādarthanirdhāraṇaṃ matiḥ |
tatra ceṣṭāstu kartavyakaraṇaṃ saṃśayachidā ||2.75||
śiṣyopadeśabhrūkṣepāvūhāpohādayo' pi ca |
jñānavijñānagurvādibhaktinānārthasiddhibhiḥ ||2.76||
lajjādibhiśca cittasya naispṛhyaṃ dhṛtirucyate |
atrānubhāvā vijñeyāḥ prāptārthānubhavastathā ||2.77||
aprāptātītanaṣṭārthānabhisaṅkṣobhaṇādayaḥ |
manorathasya lābhena siddhyā yogyasya vastunaḥ ||2.78||
mitrasaṅgamadevādiprasādādeśca kalpitaḥ |
manaḥprasādo harṣaḥ syādatra netrāsyaphullatā ||2.79||
priyābhāṣaṇamāśleṣaḥ pulakānāṃ prarohaṇam |
svedodgamaśca hastena hastasampīḍanādayaḥ ||2.80||
kālākṣamatvamautsukyamiṣṭavastuviyogataḥ |
taddarśanādramyavastudidṛkṣādeśca tatkriyāḥ ||2.81||
tvarānavasthitiḥ śayyāsthitiruttānacintane |
śarīragauravaṃ nidrātandrāniḥśvasitādayaḥ ||2.82||
aparādhāvamānābhyāṃ cauryāābhigrahaṇādibhiḥ |
asatpralāpanādyaiśca kṛtaṃ caṇḍatvamugratā ||2.83||
kriyāstatrāsyanayanarāgo bandhanatāḍane |
śirasaḥ kampanaṃ khedavadhanirbhartsanādayaḥ ||2.84||
adhikṣepāvamānādyaiḥ krodho' marṣa itīryate |
tatra svedaśiraḥkampāvādhomukhyavicintane ||2.85||
upāyānveṣaṇotsāhavyavasādayaḥ kriyāḥ |
parasaubhāgyasampattividyāśauryādihetubhiḥ ||2.86||
guṇe' pi doṣāropaḥ syādasūyā tatra vikriyāḥ |
mukhāpavartanaṃ garhā bhrūbhedānādarādayaḥ ||2.87||
rāgadveṣādibhiścittalāghavaṃ cāpalaṃ bhavet |
ceṣṭāstatrāvicāreṇa parirambhāvalambane ||2.88||
niṣkāsanoktipāruṣye tāḍanājñāpanādayaḥ |
madasvabhāvavyāyāmaniścintatvaśramādibhiḥ ||2.89||
manonimīlanaṃ nidrā ceṣṭāstatrāsyagauravam |
āghūrṇamānanetratvamaṅgānāṃ parimardanam ||2.90||
niḥśvāsocchvāsane sannagātratvaṃ netramīlanam |
śarīrasya ca saṅkoco jāḍyaṃ cetyevamādayaḥ ||2.91||
udreka eva nidrāyāḥ suptiḥ syāttatra vikriyāḥ |
indriyoparatirnetramīlanaṃ srastagātratā ||2.92||
utsvapnāyitanaiścalyaśvāsocchvāsādayo' pi ca |
svapnasparśananidhvānanidrāsampūrṇatādibhiḥ ||2.93||
prabodhaścetanāvāptiśceṣṭāstatrākṣimardanam |
śayyāyā mokṣaṇaṃ bāhuvikṣepo' ṅgulimoṭanam ||2.94||
śiraḥkaṇḍūyanaṃ cāṅgavalanaṃ caivamādayaḥ |
uttamādhamamadhyeṣu sāttvikā vyabhicāriṇaḥ ||2.95||
vibhāvairanubhāvaiśca varṇanīyā yathocitam |
udvegasnehadambherṣyāpramukhāścittavṛttayaḥ ||2.96||
ukteṣvantarbhavantīti na pṛthaktvena darśitāḥ |
vibhāvāścānubhāvāśca te bhavanti parasparam ||2.97||
kāryakāraṇabhāvastu jñeyaḥ prāyeṇa lokataḥ |
svātantryātpāratantryācca te dvidhā vyabhicāriṇaḥ ||2.98||
parapoṣakatāṃ prāptāḥ paratantrā itīritāḥ |
tadabhāve svatantrāḥ syurbhāvā iti ca te smṛtāḥ ||2.99||
ābhāsatā bhavedeṣāmanaucityapravartitām |
asatyatvādayogyatvādanaucityaṃ dvidhā bhavet ||2.100||
asatyatvakṛtaṃ tatsyādacetanagataṃ tu yat |
ayogyatvakṛtaṃ proktaṃ nīcatiryaṅnarāśrayam ||2.101||
utpattisandhiśāvalyaśāntayo vyabhicāriṇām |
daśāścatasrastatra utpattirbhāvasambhavaḥ ||2.102||
sarūpamasarūpaṃ vā bhinnakāraṇakalpitam |
bhāvadvayaṃ milati cetsa sandhiriti gīyate ||2.103||
atyārūḍhasya bhāvasya vilayaḥ śāntirucyate |
śavalatvaṃ tu bhāvānāṃ saṃmardaḥ syātparasparam ||2.104||
digantarālasañcārakīrtinā siṃhabhūbhujā |
evaṃ sañcāriṇaḥ sarve saprapañcaṃ nirūpitāḥ ||2.105||
sajātīyairvijātīyairbhāvairye tvatiraskṛtāḥ |
kṣrābdhivannayantyanyān svātmatvaṃ sthāyino hi te ||2.106||
bharatena ca te kathitā ratihāsotsāhavismayakrodhāḥ |
śoko' tha jugupsā bhayamityaṣṭau lakṣma vakṣyate teṣām ||2.107||
yūnoranyonyaviṣayā sthāyinīcchā ratirbhavet |
nisargeṇābhiyogena saṃsargeṇābhimānataḥ ||2.108||
upamādhyātmaviṣayaireṣā syāttatra vikriyāḥ |
kaṭākṣapātabhrūkṣepapriyavāgādayo matāḥ ||2.109||
bhojastu samprayogeṇa ratimanyāmudāharat |
samprayogasya śabdādiṣvantarbhāvānna tanmatam ||2.110||
aṅkurapallavakalikāpraspunaphalabhogabhāgiyaṃ kramaśaḥ |
premā mānaḥ praṇayaḥ sneho rāgo' nurāgaśca ||2.111||
sa premā bhedarahitaṃ yūnoryadbhāvabandhanam |
yattu premānubandhena svātantryāddhṛdayaṅgamam ||2.112||
bahnāti bhāvakauṭilyaṃ so' yaṃ māna itīryate |
bāhyāntaropacārairyatpremamānopakalpitaiḥ ||2.113||
badhnāti bhāvaviśrambhaṃ so' yaṃ praṇaya ucyate |
viśrambhe paramāṃ kāṣṭhāmārūḍhe darśanādibhiḥ ||2.114||
yatra dravatyantaraṅgaṃ sa sneha iti kathyate |
sa tredhā kathyate prauḍhamadhyamandavibhedataḥ ||2.115||
pravāsādibhirajñātacittavṛttau priye jane |
itarakleśakārī yaḥ sa prauḍhaḥ sneha ucyate ||2.116||
itarānubhavāpekṣāṃ sahate yaḥ sa madhyamaḥ |
dvayorekasya mānādau tadanyasya karoti yaḥ ||2.117||
naivopekṣāṃ na cāpekṣāṃ sa sneho manda ucyate |
duḥkhamapyadhikaṃ citte sukhatvenaiva rajyate ||2.118||
yena snehaprakarṣeṇa sa rāga iti gīyate |
kusumbhanīlīmañjiṣṭharāgabhedena sa tridhā ||2.119||
kusumbharāgaḥ sa jñeyo yaścitte rajyati kṣaṇāt |
atiprakāśamāno' pi kṣaṇādeva vinaśyati ||2.120||
nīlīrāgastu yaḥ sakto nāpaiti na ca dīpyate |
acireṇaiva saṃsaktaścirādapi na naśyati ||2.121||
atīva śobhate yo' sau māñjiṣṭho rāga ucyate |
rāga eva svayaṃ vedyadaśāprāptyā prakāśitaḥ ||2.122||
yāvadāśrayavṛttiścedanurāga itīritaḥ |
anye prītiṃ raterbhedmāmananti na tanmatam ||2.123||
asamprayogaviṣayā seyaṃ harṣānna bhidyate |
bhāṣaṇākṛtiveṣāṇaṃ kriyāyāśca vikārataḥ ||2.124||
laulyādeśca parasthānāmeṣāmanukṛterapi |
vikāraścetaso hāsastatra ceṣṭāḥ samīritāḥ ||2.125||
dṛṣṭervikāro nāmauṣṭhakapolaspandanādayaḥ |
śaktirdhariyasahāyādyaiḥ phalaślāghyeṣu karmasu ||2.126||
satvarā mānasī vṛttirutsāhastatra vikriyāḥ |
kālādyavekṣaṇaṃ dhairyaṃ vāgārambhādayo' pi ca ||2.127||
sahajāhāryabhedena sa dvidhā paribhāṣyate |
lokottarapadārthānāṃ tatpūrvalokanādibhiḥ ||2.128||
vistāraścetaso yastu vismayaḥ sa nigadyate |
kriyāstatrākṣivistārasādhūktipulakādayaḥ ||2.129||
vadhāvajñādibhiścittajvalanaṃ krodha īritaḥ |
eṣa tridhā bhavetkordhakoparoṣaprabhedataḥ ||2.130||
vadhacchedādiparyantaḥ krodhaḥ krūrajanāśrayaḥ |
abhyarthanāvadhiḥ prāyaḥ kopo vīrajanāśrayaḥ ||2.131||
śatrubhṛtyasuhṛtpūjyāścatvāro viṣayāstayoḥ |
muhurdaṣṭoṣṭhatā bhugnabhrukuṭīdantaghaṭṭanam ||2.132||
hastaniṣpīḍanaṃ gātrakampaḥ śastrapratīkṣaṇam |
svabhujāvekṣaṇaṃ kaṇṭhagarjādyāḥ śātravakrudhi ||2.133||
bhṛtyakrodhe tu ceṣṭāḥ syustarjanaṃ mūrdhadhananam |
nirbhartsanaṃ ca bahudhā muhurnirvarṇanādayaḥ ||2.134||
mitrakrodhe vikārāḥ syurnetrāntaḥpatadaśrutā |
tūṣṇīṃ dhyānaṃ ca naiścalyaṃ śvasitāni muhurmuhuḥ ||2.135||
maunaṃ vinamramukhatā bhugnadṛṣṭyādayo' pi ca |
pūjyakrodhe tu ceṣṭāḥ syuḥ svanindā namravaktratā ||2.136||
anuttarapradānāṅgasvedagadgadikādayaḥ |
śatrukrodhe tu ceṣṭāḥ syurbhāvagarbhitabhāṣaṇam ||2.137||
bhrūbhedaniṭilasvedakaṭākṣaāruṇimādayaḥ |
bhṛtyādikopatritaye tattatkrodhāditāḥ kriyāḥ ||2.138||
mithaḥ strīpuṃsayoreva roṣahstrīgocaraḥ punaḥ |
pratyayāvadhiratra syurvikārāḥ kuṭilekṣaṇam ||2.139||
adharasphuraṇāpāṅgarāganiḥśvasitādayaḥ |
dvedhā nigaditaḥ strīṇāṃ roṣaḥ puruṣagocaraḥ ||2.140||
sapatnīheturādyaḥ syādanyaḥ syādanyahetukaḥ |
sapatnīhetuko roṣo vipralambhe prapañcyate ||2.141||
anyahetukṛte tvatra kriyāḥ puruṣaroṣavat |
bandhuvyāpattidaurgatyadhananāśādibhiḥ kṛtaḥ ||2.142||
cittakleśabharaḥ śokastatra ceṣṭā vivarṇatā |
bāṣpodgamo mukhe śoṣaḥ stambhaniḥśvasitādayaḥ ||2.143||
uttamānamayaṃ prauḍho vibhāvairanyasaṃśritaiḥ |
ātmasthairatirūḍho' pi prāyaḥ śauryeṇa śāmyati ||2.144||
tatra ceṣṭā guṇākhyānanigūḍharuditādayaḥ |
syādeṣa mṛtiparyantaḥ svaparasthaistu madhyame ||2.145||
anativyaktaruditapramukhāstatra vikriyāḥ |
hetubhiḥ svagataireva prāyaḥ strīnīcayorayam ||2.146||
maraṇavyavasāyāntastatra bhūpariveṣṭanam |
urastāḍananirbhedapātoccai rodanādayaḥ ||2.147||
ahṛdyānāṃ padārthānāṃ darśanaśravaṇādibhiḥ |
saṅkocanaṃ yanmanasahsā jugupsātra vikriyāḥ ||2.148||
nāsāpidhānaṃ tvaritā gatirāsyavikūṇanam |
sarvāṅgadhūnanaṃ kutsā muhurniṣṭhīvanādayaḥ ||2.149||
ghṛṇā śuddhā jugupsānyā daśarūpe nirūpitā |
sā heyaśravaṇotpannajugupsāyā na bhidyate ||2.150||
bhayaṃ tu mantunā ghoradarśanaśravaṇādibhiḥ |
cittasyātīva cāñcalyaṃ tatprāyo nīcamadhyayoḥ ||2.151||
uttamasya tu jāyeta kāraṇairatilaukikaiḥ |
bhaye tu ceṣṭā vaivarṇyaṃ stabdhatvaṃ gātrakampanam ||2.152||
palāyanaṃ parāvṛtya vīkṣaṇaṃ svātmagopanam |
āsyaśoṣaṇamutkrośaśaraṇānveṣaṇādayaḥ ||2.153||
hetujāditare prokte bhaye soḍhalasūnunā |
kṛtrimaṃ tūttamagataṃ gurvādīn pratyavāstavam ||2.154||
vibhīṣikotthaṃ bālādervitrāsitakamityubhe |
tatrāntyamantarbhūtaṃ syādghoraśravaṇaje bhaye ||2.155||
bhikṣubhallūkacorādisūcanākalpitatvataḥ |
ādyaṃ tu yuktikākṣyāyāṃ bhayakakṣyāṃ na gāhate ||2.156||
gurvādisaṃnidhau yasmānnīcaiḥ sthityādisūcitam |
bhāvo vinaya eva syādatha syānnāṭake yadi ||2.157||
avahitthatayā tasya bhayatvaṃ dūrato gatam |
ato hetujamevaikaṃ bhayaṃ syāditi niścayaḥ ||2.158||
bhojenoktāḥ sthāyino' nye garvaḥ sneho dhṛtirmatiḥ |
sthāsnurevoddhatapreyaḥ śāntodāttaraseṣvapi ||2.159||
tatra sneho raterbhedastridhā cecchātmatatkṛtaḥ |
anye poṣāsahiṣṇutvānnaiva sthāyipadocitāḥ ||2.160||
tadaṣṭāveva vijñeyāḥ sthāyino munisaṃmatāḥ |
sthāyino' ṣṭau trayastriṃśaccāriṇo' ṣṭau ca sāttvikāḥ ||2.161||
evamekonapañcāśadbhāvāḥ syurmilitā ime |
evaṃ hi sthāyino bhāvān siṃhabhūpatirabhyadhāt ||2.162||
athaiṣāṃ rasarūpatvamucyate siṃhabhūbhujā |
vidvanmānasahaṃsena rasabhāvavivekinā ||2.163||
ete ca sthāyinaḥ svaiḥ svairvibhāvairvyabhicāribhiḥ |
sāttvikaiścānubhāvaiśca naṭābhinayayogataḥ ||2.164||
sākṣātkāramivānītāḥ prāpitāḥ svādurūpatām |
sāmājikānāṃ manasi prayānti rasarūpatām ||2.165||
dadhyādivyañjanadravyaiściñcādibhirathauṣadhaiḥ |
guḍādimadhuradravyairyathāyogaṃ samanvitaiḥ ||2.166||
yadvatpākaviśeṣeṇa ṣāḍavākhyo rasaḥ paraḥ |
niṣpadyate vibhāvādyaiḥ prayogeṇa tathā rasaḥ ||2.167||
so' yamānandasambhedo bhāvakairanubhūyate |
aṣṭadhā sa ca śṛṅgārahāsyavīrādbhutā api ||2.168||
raudraḥ karuṇabībhatsau bhayānaka itīritaḥ |
eṣūttarastu pūrvasmātsambhūto viṣamātsamaḥ ||2.169||
bahuvaktavyatāhetoḥ sakalāhlādanādapi |
raseṣu tatra śṛṅgāraḥ prathamaṃ lakṣyate sphuṭam ||2.170||
vibhāvairanubhāvaiśca sāttvikairvyabhicāribhiḥ |
nītā sadasyarasyatvaṃ ratiḥ śṛṅgāra ucyate ||2.171||
sa vipralambhaḥ sambhoga iti dvedhā nigadyate |
ayuktayostaruṇayoryo' nurāgaḥ parasparam ||2.172||
abhīṣṭāliṅganādīnāmanavāptau prakṛṣyate |
sa vipralambho vijñeyaḥ sa caturdhā nigadyate ||2.173||
pūrvānurāgamānau ca pravāsakaruṇāviti |
yatprema saṅgamātpūrvaṃ darśanaśravaṇādibhiḥ ||2.174||
pūrvānurāgaḥ sa jñeyaḥ śravaṇaṃ tadguṇaśrutiḥ |
pratyakṣacitrasvapnādau darśanaṃ darśanaṃ matam ||2.175||
yataḥ pūrvānurāgo' yaṃ saṅkalpātmā pravartate |
so' yaṃ pūrvānurāgākhyo vipralambha itīritaḥ ||2.176||
pāratantryādayaṃ dvedhā daivamānuṣakalpanāt |
tatra sañcāriṇo glāniḥ śaṅkāsūye śramo bhayam ||2.177||
nirvedautsukyadainyāni cintānidre prabodhatā |
viṣādo jaḍatonmādo moho maraṇameva ca ||2.178||
etasminnabhilāṣādi maraṇāntamanekadhā |
tattatsañcāribhāvānāmutkaṭatvāddaśā bhavet ||2.179||
tathāpi prāktanairasyā daśāvasthāḥ samāsataḥ |
proktāstadanurodhena tāsāṃ lakṣaṇamucyate ||2.180||
abhilāṣaścintānusmṛtiguṇasaṅkīrtanodvegāḥ |
savilāpā unmādavyādhī jaḍatā mṛtiśca tāḥ kramaśaḥ ||2.181||
saṅgamopāyaracitaprārabdhavyavasāyataḥ |
saṅkalpecchāsamudbhūtirabhilāṣo' tra vikriyāḥ ||2.182||
praveśanirgamau tūṣṇīṃ taddṛṣṭipathagāminau |
rāgaprakāśanaparāśceṣṭāḥ svātmaprasādhanam ||2.183||
vyājoktayaśca vijane sthitirityevamādayaḥ |
kenopāyena saṃsiddhiḥ kadā tasya samāgamaḥ ||2.184||
dūtīmukhena kiṃ vācyamityādyūhastu cintanam |
atra nīvyādisaṃsparśaḥ śayyāyāṃ parivartanam ||2.185||
sabāṣpākekarā dṛṣṭirmudrikādivivartanam |
nirlakṣyavīkṣaṇaṃ caivamādyā vikṛtayo matāḥ ||2.186||
arthānāmanubhūtānāṃ deśakālānuvartinām |
sāntatyena parāmarśo mānasaḥ syādanusmṛtiḥ ||2.187||
tatrānubhāvā niḥśvāso dhyāṇaṃ kṛtyavihastatā |
śayyāsanādividveṣa ityādyāḥ smarakalpitāḥ ||2.188||
saundaryādiguṇaślāghā guṇakīrtanamatra tu |
romāñco gadgadā vāṇī bhāvamantharavīkṣaṇam ||2.189||
tatsaṅgacintanaṃ sakhyā gaṇḍasvedādayo' pi ca |
manasaḥ kampa udvegaḥ kathitastatra vikriyāḥ ||2.190||
cintā santāpaniḥśvāsau dveṣaḥ śayyāsanādiṣu |
stambhacintāśruvaivarṇyadīnatvādaya īritāḥ ||2.191||
iha me dṛkpathaṃ prāpadihātiṣṭhadihāsta ca |
ihālapadihāvātsīdihaiva nyavṛtattathā ||2.192||
ityādivākyavinyāso vilāpa iti kīrtitaḥ |
tatra ceṣṭāstu kutrāpi gamanaṃ kvacidīkṣaṇam ||2.193||
kvacitkvacidavasthānaṃ kvacicca bhramaṇādayaḥ |
sarvāvasthāsu sarvatra tanmanaskatayā sadā ||2.194||
atasmiṃstaditi bhrāntirunmādo virahodbhavaḥ |
tatra ceṣṭāstu vijñeyā dveṣaḥ sveṣṭe' pi vastuni ||2.195||
dīrghaṃ muhuśca niḥśvāso nirnimeṣatayā sthitiḥ |
nirnimittasmitadhyānagānamaunādayo' pi ca ||2.196||
abhīṣṭasaṅgamābhāvādvyādhiḥ santāpalakṣaṇaḥ |
atra santāpaniḥśvāsau śītavastuniṣevaṇam ||2.197||
jīvitopekṣaṇaṃ moho mumūrṣā dhṛtivarjanam |
yatra kvacicca patanaṃ srastākṣatvādayo' pi ca ||2.198||
idamiṣṭamaniṣṭaṃ taditi vetti na kiñcana |
nottaraṃ bhāṣate praśne nekṣate na śṛṇoti ca ||2.199||
yatra dhyāyati niḥsaṃjñaṃ jaḍatā sā prakīrtitā |
atra sparśānabhijñatvaṃ vaivarṇyaṃ śithilāṅgatā ||2.200||
akāṇḍahuṅkṛtiḥ stambho niḥśvāsakṛśatādayaḥ |
taistaiḥ kṛtaiḥ pratīkārairyadi na syātsamāgamaḥ ||2.201||
tataḥ syānmaraṇodyogahkāmāgnestatra vikriyāḥ |
līlāśukacakorādinyāsaḥ snigdhasakhīkare ||2.202||
kalakaṇṭhakalālāpaśrutirmandānilādaraḥ |
jyotsnāpraveśamākandamañjarīvīkṣaṇādayaḥ ||2.203||
muhuḥkṛto meti neti pratiṣedhārthavīpsayā |
īpsitāliṅganādīnāṃ nirodho māna ucyate ||2.204||
so' yaṃ sahetunirhetubhedāddvedhātra hetujaḥ |
īrṣyayā sambhavedīrṣyā tvanyāsaṅgini vallabhe ||2.205||
asahiṣṇutvameva syāddṛṣṭeranumiteḥ śruteḥ |
īrṣyāmāne tu nirvedāvahitthaglānidīnatāḥ ||2.206||
cintācāpalyajaḍatāmohādyā vyabhicāriṇaḥ |
bhogāṅkagotraskhalanotsvapnairanumitistridhā ||2.207||
śrutiḥ priyāparādhasya śrutirāptasakhīmukhāt |
kāraṇābhāsasambhūto nirhetuḥ syāddvayorapi ||2.208||
avahitthādayastatra vijñeyā vyabhicāriṇaḥ |
nirhetukaḥ svayaṃ śāmyetsvayaṃ grāhasmitādibhiḥ ||2.209||
hetujastu śamaṃ yāti yathāyogyaṃ prakalpitaiḥ |
sāmnā bhedena dānena natyupekṣārasāntaraiḥ ||2.210||
tatra priyoktikathanaṃ yattu tatsāma gīyate |
sakhyādibhirupālambhaprayogo bheda ucyate ||2.211||
vyājena bhūṣaṇādīnāṃ pradānaṃ dānamucyate |
natiḥ pādapraṇāmaḥ syāttūṣṇīṃ sthitirupekṣaṇam ||2.212||
ākasmikabhayādīnāṃ kalpanā syadrasāntaram |
yādṛcchikaṃ buddhipūrvamiti dvedhā nigadyate ||2.213||
anukūlena daivena kṛtaṃ yādṛcchikaṃ bhavet |
pratyutpannadhiyā puṃsā kalpitaṃ buddhipūrvakam ||2.214||
pūrvasaṅgatayoryūnorbhaveddeśāntarādibhiḥ |
caraṇavyavadhānaṃ yatsa pravāsa itīryate ||2.215||
tajjanyo vipralambho' pi pravāsatvena saṃmataḥ |
harṣagarvamadavrīḍā varjayitvā samīritāḥ ||2.216||
śṛṅgārayogyāḥ sarve' pi pravāsavyabhicāriṇaḥ |
kāryataḥ sambhramācchāpātsa tridhā tatra kāryajaḥ ||2.217||
buddhipūrvatayā yūnoḥ saṃvidhānavyapekṣayā |
vṛtto vartiṣyamāṇaśca vartamāna iti tridhā ||2.218||
āvegaḥ sambhramaḥ so' pi naiko divyādibhedataḥ |
śāpo vairūpyatādrūpyapravṛtterdvividho bhavet ||2.219||
pravāsaḥ śāpavairūpyādahalyāgautamādiṣu |
dvayorekasya maraṇe punarujjīvanāvadhau ||2.220||
virahaḥ karuṇo' nyasya saṅgamāśānuvartanāt |
karuṇabhramakāritvātso' yaṃ karuṇa ucyate ||2.221||
sañcāriṇo' nubhāvāśca karuṇe' pi pravāsavat |
sparśanāliṅganādīnāmānukūlyānniṣevaṇam ||2.222||
ghaṭate yatra yūnoryatsa sambhogaścaturvidhaḥ |
saṅkṣiptaḥ saṅkīrṇaḥ sampannataraḥ samṛddhimāniti te ||2.223||
pūrvānurāgamānapravāsakaruṇānusambhavāḥ kramataḥ |
yuvānau yatra saṃkṣiptān sādhvasavrīḍitādibhiḥ ||2.224||
upacārānniṣevete sa saṃkṣipta itīritaḥ |
saṅkīrṇastu parādhīna vyalīkamaraṇādibhiḥ ||2.225||
saṅkīryamāṇaḥ sambhogaḥ kiñcitpuṣpeṣupeśalaḥ |
bhayavyalīkasmaraṇādyabhāvātprāptavaibhavaḥ ||2.226||
proṣitāgatayoryūnorbhogaḥ sampanna īritaḥ |
punarujjīvane bhogasamṛddhiḥ kiyatī bhavet ||2.227||
śivābhyāmeva vijñeyamityayaṃ hi samṛddhimān |
vibhāvairanubhāvaiśca svocitairvyabhicāribhiḥ ||2.228||
hāsaḥ sadasyarasyatvaṃ nīto hāsya itīryate |
tatrālasyaglāninidrāvyādhyādyā vyabhicāriṇaḥ ||2.229||
eṣa dvedhā bhavedātmaparasthitivibhāgataḥ |
ātmasthastu yadā svasya vikārairhasati svayam ||2.230||
parasthastu paraprāptairetairhasati cetparam |
prakṛtivaśātsa ca ṣoḍhā smitahasite vihasitāvahasite ca ||2.231||
apahasitātihasitake jyeṣṭhādīnāṃ kramāddve dve |
smitaṃ cālakṣyadaśanaṃ dṛkkapolavikāśakṛt ||2.232||
tadeva lakṣyadaśanaśikharaṃ hasitaṃ bhavet |
tadeva kuñcitāpāṅgagaṇḍaṃ madhuraniḥsvanam ||2.233||
kālocitaṃ sānurāgamuktaṃ vihasitaṃ bhavet |
phullanāsāpuṭaṃ yatsyānnikuñcitaśiro' ṃsakam ||2.234||
jihmāvalokanayanaṃ taccāvahasitaṃ matam |
kampitāṅgaṃ sāśrunetraṃ taccāpahasitaṃ bhavet ||2.235||
karopagūḍhapārśvaṃ yaduddhatāyataniḥsvanam |
bāṣpākulākṣayugalaṃ taccātihasitaṃ bhavet ||2.236||
vibhāvairanubhāvaiśca svocitairvyabhicāribhiḥ |
nītaḥ sadasyarasyatvamutsāho vīra ucyate ||2.237||
eṣa tridhā samāsena dānayuddhadayodbhavāḥ |
dānavīro dhṛtirharṣo matyādyā vyabhicāriṇaḥ ||2.238||
smitapūrvābhibhāṣitvaṃ smitapūrvaṃ ca vīkṣitam |
prasāde bahudātṛtvaṃ tadvadvācānumoditam ||2.239||
guṇāguṇavicārādyāstvanubhāvāḥ samīritāḥ |
yuddhavīre harṣagarvāmarṣādayā vyabhicāriṇā ||2.240||
asāhāyye' pi yuddhecchā samarādapalāyanam |
bhītābhayapradānādyā vikārāstatra kīrtitāḥ ||2.241||
dayāvīre dhṛtimatipramukhā vyabhicāriṇaḥ |
svārthaprāṇavyayenāpi vipannatrāṇaśīlatā ||2.242||
āśvāsanoktayaḥ sthairyamityādyāstatra vikriyāḥ |
vibhāvairanubhāvaiśca svocitairvyabhicāribhiḥ ||2.243||
nītaḥ sadasyarasyatvaṃ vismayo' dbhutatāṃ vrajet |
atra dhṛtyāvegajāḍyaharṣādyā vyabhicāriṇaḥ ||2.244||
ceṣṭāstu netravistārasvedāśrupulakādayaḥ |
vibhāvairanubhāvaiśca svocitairvyabhicāribhiḥ ||2.245||
krodhaḥ sadasyarasyatvaṃ nīto raudra itīryate |
āvegagarvaugryāmarṣamohādyā vyabhicāriṇaḥ ||2.246||
prasvedabhrukuṭīnetrarāgādyāstatra vikriyāḥ |
vibhāvairanubhāvaiśca svocitairvyabhicāribhiḥ ||2.247||
nītaḥ sadasyarasyatvaṃ śokaḥ karuṇa ucyate |
atrāṣṭau sāttvikā jāḍyanirvedaglānidīnatāḥ ||2.248||
ālasyāpasmṛtivyādhimohādyā vyabhicāriṇaḥ |
vibhāvairanubhāvaiśca svocitairvyabhicāribhiḥ ||2.249||
jugupsā poṣamāpannā bībhatsatvena rasyate |
atra glāniśramonmādamohāpasmāradīnatāḥ ||2.250||
viṣādacāpalāvegajāḍyādyā vyabhicāriṇaḥ |
svedaromāñcanāmāgracchādanādyāśca vikriyāḥ ||2.251||
vibhāvairanubhāvaiśca svocitairvyabhicāribhiḥ |
bhayaṃ sadasyarasyatvaṃ nītaṃ proktaṃ bhayānakaḥ ||2.252||
tatra santrāsamaraṇacāpalāvegadīnatāḥ |
viṣādamohāpasmāraśaṅkādyā vyabhicāriṇaḥ ||2.253||
vikriyāstvāsyaśoṣādyāḥ sāttvikāścāśruvarjitāḥ |
kecitsamānabalayo rasayoḥ saṅkaraṃ viduḥ ||2.254||
na parīkṣākṣamamidaṃ mataṃ prekṣāvatāṃ bhavet |
tuṣyatve pūrva āsvādaḥ katarasyetyaniścayāt ||2.255||
spardhāparatvādubhayoranāsvādaprasaṅgataḥ |
tayoranyatarasyaiva prāyeṇāsvādanādapi ||2.256||
yugapadrasanīyatvaṃ nobhayorupapadyate |
eṣāmaṅgāṅgibhāvena saṅkaro mama saṃmataḥ ||2.257||

[*1] anotherreadingin some manuscriptsanubhāvastu naiṣphalyamatirnirveda ucyate | atra cintāśruniḥśvasavaivarṇyocchvāsadīnatā ||
[*2] the followinghalf karika isfoundinonlyone edition (śītajvare tu ceṣṭāḥ syuḥ santāpaścāṅgasādanam |)


tathā ca bhāratīye
bhāvo vāpi raso vāpi pravṛttirvṛttireva vā |
sarveṣāṃ samavetānāṃ rūpaṃ yasya bhavedbahu ||
sa mantavyo rasaḥ sthāyī śeṣāḥ sañcāriṇo matāḥ ||iti||

tulādhṛtatvamanayorna syātprakaraṇādinā |
kavitātparyaviśrānterekatraivāvalokanāt ||2.258||
ubhau śṛṅgārabībhatsāvubhau vīrabhayānakau |
raudrādbhutāvubhau hāsyakaruṇau prakṛtidviṣau ||2.259||
svabhāvavairiṇoraṅgāṅgibhāvenāpi miśraṇam |
vivekibhyo na svadate gandhagandhakayoriva ||2.260||
virodhino' pi sāṃnidhyādatiraskāralakṣaṇam |
poṣaṇaṃ prakṛtasyeti cedaṅgatvaṃ na tāvatā ||2.261||
yatkiñcidupakāritvādaṅgasyāṅgitvamaṅgini |
na tatsaṃnidhimātreṇa carvaṇānupakārataḥ ||2.262||
anyathā pānakādyeṣu śarkarāderivāpatet |
antarā patitasyāpi tṛṇāderupakāritā ||2.263||
taccarvaṇābhimāne syātsatṛṇābhyavahāritā |
bhṛtyornāyakasyeva nisargadveṣiṇorapi ||2.264||
aṅgayoraṅgino vṛddhau bhavedekatra saṅgatiḥ |
aṅgenāṅgī rasaḥ svecchāvṛttivardhitasampadā ||2.265||
amātyenāvinītena svāmīvābhāsatāṃ vrajet ||2.266||

hariścandro rakṣākaraṇarucisatyeṣu vacasāṃ
vilāse vāgīśo mahati niyame nītinigame |
vijetā gāṅgeyaṃ janabharaṇasaṃmohanakalā
vrateṣu śrīsiṃhakṣitipatirudāro viharate ||2.267||

nitya śrīyannapotakṣitipatijanuṣaḥ siṃhabhūpālamauleḥ
saundaryaṃ sundarīṇāṃ hariṇavijayināṃ vāgurā locanānām |
dānaṃ mandāracintāmaṇisurasurabhīgarvanirvāpaṇāṅkaṃ
vijñānaṃ sarvavidyānidhibudhapariṣacchemuṣībhāgyarekhā ||2.268||


iti śrīmadāndhramaṇḍalādhīśvarapratigaṇḍabhairavaśrīmadanapotanarendranandanabhujabalabhīmaśrīsiṃhabhūpālaviracite rasārṇavasudhākaranāmni nāṭyālaṅkāraśāstre rasikollāso nāma
dvitīyo vilāsaḥ
||3.2||


ḥ

(3)
tṛtīyo vilāsaḥ
bhāvakollāsaḥ

sa kṣemadāyī kamalānukūlaḥ
kaṭhorapārāvatakaṇṭhanīlaḥ |
kṛpānidhirbhavyaguṇābhirāmaḥ
paraḥ pumān rajamahīdhravāsī ||1||

tadīdṛśarasādhāraṃ nāṭyaṃ rūpakamityapi |
naṭasyātipravīṇasya karmatvānnāṭyamucyate ||2||
yathā mukhādau padmāderārope rūpakaprathā |
tathaiva nāyakāropo naṭe rūpakamucyate ||3.3||
tacca nāṭyaṃ daśavidhaṃ vākyārthābhinayātmakam |

tathā ca bhāratīye
nāṭakaṃ saprakaraṇamaṅko vyāyokga eva ca |
bhāṇaḥ samavakāraśca vīthī prahasanaṃ ḍimaḥ ||
īhāmṛgaśca vijñeyo daśadhā nāṭyalakṣaṇam | (18.23)
